# CODE_Q Domain Rows — Batch ES-2026-05-08-CODE_Q

Compact DPO rows for the CODE_Q adapter. Per DPO authoring guidance §"BUG
domain — most common confusion", CODE_Q had **0 of 4 batches with
positive reward margin** in calibration. The rejected for every CODE_Q
prompt must be an **action response** (BUG fix or PLAN step) — not
another explanation, not a negation.

## Common coverage labels (apply to every row in this file)

```
domain_label:         CODE_Q
source_license_state: external_license_checked
contract_signature:   AdaptiveLogicalOrchestrator (routing) + FormatterUnit (response)
node_role:            row_level_pair
pair_kind:            flow_node
coverage_status:      covered
attribution_required: false
transition_type:      explain    (every row in this file)
```

Format: `[ID | type/OR/sub] sources | A | B | P | R`

CODE_Q intent verbs:
- "How does X work"
- "What is the difference between"
- "Where is X defined"
- "Why does X behave that way"

Rejected pattern (Type C — wrong domain): the rejected misreads the verb
and routes to BUG (fix) or PLAN (build) instead of explanation.

---

## §1 — "How does X work" — JWT / OAuth / RFC explanations

[CODEQ-0001 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does the JWT iss claim get validated against the OpenID Connect issuer URL?" | B:explanation citing RFC 7519 §4.1.1 + OIDC Discovery 1.0 — string equality after fetching issuer's discovery document | P:BUG/4.1 fix the issuer-validation code path | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how it works, no defect named]
[CODEQ-0002 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519+rfc-8725 | A:"How does the alg=none rejection rule from RFC 8725 work in practice?" | B:explanation: validators must build an alg-allowlist before signature verification; alg=none is a header value, must reject | P:BUG/4.1 fix the validator | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how it works]
[CODEQ-0003 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-6749 | A:"How does PKCE work for public clients?" | B:explanation: code_verifier per session, code_challenge=BASE64URL(SHA256(verifier)) sent in /authorize, server stores, on /token verifies match | P:PLAN/4.1 implement PKCE | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how it works]
[CODEQ-0004 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-7519+rfc-7515 | A:"How does the kid header in JWS work for key rotation?" | B:explanation: kid is a hint pointing to the JWK; rotation is overlapping windows where both old and new kid resolve | P:PLAN/4.1 implement rotation | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how it works]
[CODEQ-0005 | C/0.23/3.4] rfc-6749+rfc-8693 | A:"How does OAuth token exchange differ from the regular authorization flow?" | B:explanation: token exchange is a grant_type, takes existing token + actor_token, returns new subject_token with different aud or scope | P:PLAN/4.1 implement | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how]
[CODEQ-0006 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-6749 | A:"How does refresh token rotation prevent replay?" | B:explanation: each use returns new refresh + invalidates old; replay of consumed refresh is detected by lookup miss | P:PLAN/4.1 implement | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how]

[CODEQ-0008 | C/0.19/3.3] rfc-9110+rfc-7234 | A:"How do Cache-Control max-age and stale-while-revalidate work together?" | B:explanation citing RFC 5861: max-age sets fresh window; SWR allows serving stale during revalidation | P:PLAN/4.1 implement caching | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how]
[CODEQ-0009 | C/0.21/3.3] rfc-9110 | A:"How does HTTP/3 use QUIC for transport?" | B:explanation: QUIC over UDP, integrated TLS 1.3, connection migration via connection ID | P:PLAN/4.3 deploy HTTP/3 | R:[WRONG: prompt asks how]

[CODEQ-0097 | C/0.21/5.2] testcontainers-dotnet | A:"How does Testcontainers' container network sharing enable multi-container tests?" | B:explanation: shared docker network; containers reach each other by alias | P:PLAN/5.2 use | R:[WRONG: how]

[CODEQ-0101 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-6749+rfc-7519 | A:"What is the difference between an OAuth access token and an OIDC id_token?" | B:explanation: access=opaque or JWT for resource access; id_token=JWT with user-claims for client | P:PLAN/4.1 implement | R:[WRONG: difference question]
[CODEQ-0102 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-6749 | A:"What is the difference between an OAuth scope and an OAuth audience?" | B:explanation: scope=permissions per token; aud=intended resource server | P:PLAN/4.1 implement | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0103 | C/0.22/3.3] openapi-3.1 | A:"What is the difference between an OpenAPI parameter (in=body) and the OpenAPI 3.x requestBody?" | B:explanation: 2.0 used parameter in=body; 3.x dedicated requestBody object with per-content-type schemas | P:PLAN/4.5 migrate | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0104 | C/0.19/3.3] rfc-7807 | A:"What is the difference between application/problem+json and a custom JSON error format?" | B:explanation: RFC 7807 standardised type/title/status/detail/instance — interoperable; custom formats fragmentate clients | P:PLAN/4.1 migrate | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0105 | C/0.21/3.5] temporal+dapr | A:"What is the difference between Temporal and Dapr workflow approaches?" | B:explanation: Temporal=stateful workflow service with replay; Dapr=lightweight orchestrations atop building blocks | P:PLAN/4.5 select | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0106 | C/0.20/3.5] temporal+airflow | A:"What is the difference between Temporal and Airflow for long-running workflows?" | B:explanation: Temporal=code-defined, replay-based, sub-second activities; Airflow=DAGs at minutes-to-hours, batch | P:PLAN/4.5 select | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0107 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-7519+rfc-8725 | A:"What is the difference between RFC 7519 and RFC 8725?" | B:explanation: 7519=spec; 8725=BCP with security recommendations (alg allowlist, kid mandatory, etc.) | P:PLAN/4.1 align | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0108 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-6749+rfc-8252 | A:"What is the difference between RFC 6749 and RFC 8252?" | B:explanation: 6749=core OAuth; 8252=BCP for native apps (PKCE, custom URI schemes, loopback redirects) | P:PLAN/4.1 align | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0109 | C/0.21/5.2] testcontainers-dotnet+testfx | A:"What is the difference between Testcontainers and standard test fixtures?" | B:explanation: standard fixtures mock external deps; Testcontainers spins real services for cross-component verification | P:PLAN/5.2 select | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0110 | C/0.20/3.4] cwe-285+cwe-287 | A:"What is the difference between CWE-285 and CWE-287?" | B:explanation: 285=authenticated but unauthorized for resource; 287=unauthenticated access | P:PLAN/6.3 review | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0111 | C/0.22/3.4] cwe-89+cwe-943 | A:"What is the difference between CWE-89 (SQL) and CWE-943 (NoSQL)?" | B:explanation: 89=SQL injection via concatenation; 943=NoSQL injection (e.g. $regex operator abuse) — same root cause, different syntax | P:BUG/4.1 fix both | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0112 | C/0.19/3.4] cwe-79+cwe-1021 | A:"What is the difference between XSS (CWE-79) and Clickjacking (CWE-1021)?" | B:explanation: XSS=script injection; Clickjacking=UI redress via overlaid frame | P:BUG/4.2 fix both | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0113 | C/0.21/3.4] cwe-352+cwe-918 | A:"What is the difference between CSRF (CWE-352) and SSRF (CWE-918)?" | B:explanation: CSRF=victim's browser tricked into request; SSRF=server-side request to attacker-supplied URL | P:BUG/4.1 fix both | R:[WRONG: difference]
[CODEQ-0114 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-9110+rfc-7234 | A:"What is the difference between Cache-Control: no-store and no-cache?" | B:explanation: no-store=do not store; no-cache=may store but must revalidate before serve | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: difference]

[CODEQ-0181 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7521 | A:"How does OAuth assertion framework allow SAML or JWT bearer grants?" | B:explanation: assertion-based grants exchange a signed assertion for access token; replaces user/pass for service principals | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0182 | C/0.22/3.5] temporal | A:"What is the difference between Temporal's local activities and regular activities?" | B:explanation: local=in-worker process, lower latency, no separate queue; regular=remote, durable | P:PLAN/4.1 select | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0183 | C/0.19/3.5] dapr | A:"How does Dapr's tracing context propagate through pub/sub?" | B:explanation: trace context attached to message metadata; consumer SDK extracts and continues | P:PLAN/4.5 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0184 | C/0.21/3.5] airflow-dag | A:"Why does Airflow's task retry interval default to 5 minutes?" | B:explanation: balanced for typical batch ETL; transient errors common in distributed jobs | P:PLAN/4.5 tune | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0185 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"What is the difference between the JWT 'cnf' claim methods (jkt vs x5t#S256)?" | B:explanation: jkt=JWK SHA-256 thumbprint; x5t#S256=X.509 cert SHA-256 thumbprint | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0186 | C/0.22/3.4] cwe-352 | A:"Where does CWE-352 mitigation guidance rank token-based vs origin-based defenses?" | B:explanation: token=primary mitigation; origin=defense-in-depth; recommend both | P:BUG/4.2 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0187 | C/0.19/3.4] cwe-918 | A:"How does CWE-918 mitigation guidance rank SSRF validation strategies?" | B:explanation: deny-by-default + allowlist; canonicalise; resolve+pin IP per RFC 1918; audit egress at firewall | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0188 | C/0.21/3.4] cwe-22 | A:"Why does POSIX.1 chroot suit CWE-22 path-traversal mitigation for high-risk endpoints?" | B:explanation: chroot=kernel-enforced jail; impossible to escape via path tricks | P:PLAN/4.4 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0189 | C/0.20/3.4] cwe-1021 | A:"How does the COOP header (Cross-Origin Opener Policy) extend clickjacking defenses?" | B:explanation: COOP=same-origin isolates browsing context group; mitigates Spectre+XS-Leaks | P:BUG/4.2 add | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0190 | C/0.22/3.5] temporal | A:"How does Temporal's signal-with-start handle a race?" | B:explanation: atomic operation — workflow created if absent, signal queued | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0191 | C/0.19/3.5] dapr | A:"What is the difference between Dapr's pub/sub bulk publish and individual publish?" | B:explanation: bulk=single call sends N messages, single broker round-trip; individual=N round-trips | P:PLAN/4.5 select | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0192 | C/0.21/3.5] airflow-dag | A:"Where does Airflow document the DAG-level pool override?" | B:explanation: BaseOperator(pool=name); per-task override of DAG pool | P:PLAN/4.5 configure | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0193 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does the JWT validation order recommended by RFC 8725 work?" | B:explanation: parse → algorithm allowlist check → signature verify → claim checks (iss/aud/exp/nbf) | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]

[CODEQ-0195 | C/0.19/3.3] openapi-3.1 | A:"Why does OpenAPI 3.1 use an array of types for nullability?" | B:explanation: aligns with JSON Schema 2020-12 — type can be array including 'null' | P:PLAN/4.5 migrate | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]

[CODEQ-0197 | C/0.20/3.4] cwe-285 | A:"What is the difference between authentication and authorization in CWE taxonomy?" | B:explanation: auth-n=identify; auth-z=permit specific action on resource | P:PLAN/6.3 review | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0198 | C/0.22/3.4] cwe-918 | A:"How does cloud metadata-service abuse via SSRF (e.g., 169.254.169.254) work?" | B:explanation: instance metadata returns IAM credentials; SSRF reaches it from VM | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0199 | C/0.19/3.5] temporal | A:"Where does Temporal document its workflow-task heartbeating?" | B:explanation: docs/concepts/workers — workers heartbeat task with `RecordHeartbeat` | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0200 | C/0.21/3.5] dapr | A:"How does Dapr handle Kubernetes Service Mesh integration vs Dapr's mTLS?" | B:explanation: Dapr-to-Dapr mTLS is independent of mesh; can coexist; pick one for control plane | P:PLAN/4.5 configure | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0201 | C/0.20/3.5] testcontainers-dotnet | A:"What is the difference between Testcontainers' GenericContainer and the modular containers?" | B:explanation: GenericContainer=any image; modular (PostgresContainer, etc.)=preconfigured wait strategies | P:PLAN/5.2 select | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0202 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does the JWT confusion attack (RS to HS) actually exploit?" | B:explanation: validator looks up key; if it accepts public key as HMAC secret, attacker signs HS256 with public key | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0203 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-6749 | A:"Why does PKCE replace client_secret for public clients?" | B:explanation: public clients (mobile, SPA) cannot keep secret confidential; PKCE proves origination via code_verifier | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0204 | C/0.21/3.5] temporal-saga | A:"How does Temporal saga handle partial failures during compensation?" | B:explanation: each compensation idempotent; if compensation fails, retried per policy; configurable timeouts | P:PLAN/4.1 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0205 | C/0.20/3.5] dapr | A:"What is the difference between Dapr's state store transactional API and individual writes?" | B:explanation: transactional=atomic batch (all-or-nothing); individual=independent writes | P:PLAN/4.5 select | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0206 | C/0.22/3.5] airflow-dag | A:"How does Airflow's sensor reschedule mode reduce worker pressure?" | B:explanation: reschedule=releases worker between pokes; runs as queued tasks; vs poke=holds worker | P:PLAN/4.5 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0207 | C/0.19/3.4] cwe-285 | A:"What is the difference between role-based and attribute-based access control?" | B:explanation: RBAC=roles map to permissions; ABAC=policies evaluate attributes (subject, action, resource, environment) | P:PLAN/6.3 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0208 | C/0.21/3.4] cwe-79 | A:"How does Trusted Types API in CSP3 prevent DOM XSS?" | B:explanation: enforces typed objects for sink-injection points; raw strings rejected at API boundary | P:BUG/4.2 add | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0209 | C/0.20/3.4] cwe-918 | A:"Why is the cloud-IMDSv2 token recommended for SSRF mitigation?" | B:explanation: IMDSv2 requires PUT to acquire token; SSRF typically only does GET, blocked | P:BUG/4.3 enable | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0210 | C/0.22/3.4] cwe-22 | A:"How does Java's Path::resolve(...).normalize() affect path traversal validation?" | B:explanation: normalize removes . and .. segments; combined with resolve produces canonical | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0211 | C/0.19/3.4] cwe-611 | A:"Why does .NET's XmlReader default to disabling external entities?" | B:explanation: secure-by-default since .NET Framework 4.5.2; older versions required explicit DtdProcessing.Prohibit | P:BUG/4.1 verify | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0212 | C/0.21/3.5] temporal | A:"What is the difference between Temporal cluster and namespace?" | B:explanation: cluster=physical deployment; namespace=logical isolation within cluster | P:PLAN/4.1 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0213 | C/0.20/3.5] dapr | A:"How does Dapr's actor reminders differ from timers?" | B:explanation: reminders=durable, survive actor deactivation; timers=in-memory, lost on deactivation | P:PLAN/4.5 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0214 | C/0.22/5.2] testcontainers-dotnet | A:"How does Testcontainers' DockerImage tag selection interact with image-pull policy?" | B:explanation: explicit tag = no pull if local; latest = always pull; configurable per container | P:PLAN/5.2 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0215 | C/0.19/5.3] playwright | A:"What is the difference between Playwright's locator() and page.$()?" | B:explanation: locator=lazy reference, re-resolves on each action; $()=immediate query, may go stale | P:PLAN/5.3 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0216 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does the JWT 'jti' uniqueness requirement scale across distributed validators?" | B:explanation: shared store (Redis) with TTL = exp; each validator checks-then-sets | P:PLAN/4.1 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0217 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-6749 | A:"What is the difference between OAuth scope and OpenID claims?" | B:explanation: scope=permissions on access token; claims=user-info attributes (returned via id_token or userinfo) | P:PLAN/4.1 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0218 | C/0.22/3.5] temporal | A:"Why does Temporal recommend small workflow histories?" | B:explanation: replay performance scales with history size; large histories slow workers and increase RAM | P:PLAN/4.1 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]

[CODEQ-0223 | C/0.19/3.4] cwe-352 | A:"What is the difference between origin-header and same-origin-cookie validation?" | B:explanation: origin=request header showing requesting origin; same-origin-cookie=browser-enforced cookie scope | P:BUG/4.2 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]

[CODEQ-0235 | C/0.19/5.3] playwright | A:"What is the difference between Playwright's projects and config-multiple?" | B:explanation: projects=per-config test grouping in same suite; config-multiple=separate configs per browser/device | P:PLAN/5.3 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0236 | C/0.21/3.4] cwe-285 | A:"Why does CWE-285 differentiate authorization-bypass from authorization-bypass-by-spoofing-trusted-data?" | B:explanation: bypass=missing check; spoofing=check uses trustable data the attacker controls (e.g., client-side role) | P:PLAN/6.3 review | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0237 | C/0.20/3.4] cwe-287 | A:"How does CWE-287's improper-authentication-by-cleartext-credentials apply to API tokens?" | B:explanation: token transmitted in plain HTTP or stored in cleartext = improper auth; require TLS + secure storage | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]

[CODEQ-0239 | C/0.19/3.4] cwe-22 | A:"What is the difference between absolute vs relative path traversal in CWE-22?" | B:explanation: absolute=starts with /; relative=uses .. — both must be normalized + allowlisted | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]

[CODEQ-0245 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does the JWT 'kid' header field aid signing-key identification?" | B:explanation: kid points to JWK in JWKs document; allows multiple keys per issuer for rotation | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]
[CODEQ-0246 | C/0.22/3.5] temporal-saga | A:"What is the difference between Temporal saga's parallel and sequential compensation execution?" | B:explanation: parallel=all compensations fire simultaneously (faster but ordering issues if dependent); sequential=one at a time (safer for inter-dependent steps) | P:PLAN/4.1 design | R:[WRONG: explanation question — not a P: dispatch task]

## §4 — Per-language and tooling explanations (CODE_Q)

[CODEQ-0251 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-7519+microsoft-docs | A:"How does System.IdentityModel.Tokens.Jwt validate the iss claim by default?" | B:explanation: ValidIssuer / ValidIssuers list, exact match | P:BUG/4.1 fix issuer mismatch | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0252 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519+microsoft-docs | A:"How does ASP.NET Core JwtBearerHandler obtain the signing key?" | B:explanation: TokenValidationParameters.IssuerSigningKey or IssuerSigningKeyResolver delegate | P:PLAN/4.1 implement | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0253 | C/0.22/3.5] dapr-docs | A:"How does Dapr's component ConfigMap reload work in Kubernetes?" | B:explanation: dapr-operator watches ConfigMap; updates trigger sidecar reconfiguration | P:PLAN/4.5 configure | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0254 | C/0.19/3.5] temporal-docs | A:"How does Temporal's workflow worker poll task queue?" | B:explanation: long-poll for tasks; activity-task and workflow-task queues separate | P:PLAN/4.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0255 | C/0.21/3.5] testcontainers-dotnet | A:"How does Testcontainers resolve image-pull credentials?" | B:explanation: docker config from $HOME/.docker/config.json or DOCKER_AUTH_CONFIG env | P:PLAN/5.2 configure | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0256 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519+jose-docs | A:"How does jose-jwt library handle alg=none rejection?" | B:explanation: explicit algorithm allowlist on Decode call; alg=none not in allowlist by default | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0257 | C/0.22/3.3] openapi-3.1+nswag-docs | A:"How does NSwag generate C# client from OpenAPI?" | B:explanation: NSwag.MSBuild reads OpenAPI doc, generates partial classes per operation | P:PLAN/4.5 configure | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0258 | C/0.19/3.5] dapr-docs | A:"How does Dapr's pub/sub adopt at-most-once semantics?" | B:explanation: configurable per-subscription with ackTimeout=0 | P:PLAN/4.5 configure | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0259 | C/0.21/5.2] testcontainers-dotnet | A:"How does Testcontainers' WaitUntilContainerIsHealthy strategy work?" | B:explanation: polls docker inspect until Health.Status=='healthy'; uses image's HEALTHCHECK | P:PLAN/5.2 configure | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0260 | C/0.20/3.5] playwright-docs | A:"How does Playwright handle cookie persistence across BrowserContexts?" | B:explanation: context.storageState() exports; new context.storageState() option imports | P:PLAN/5.3 configure | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-0261 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-6749+ms-identity-docs | A:"How does MSAL.NET handle silent token refresh?" | B:explanation: AcquireTokenSilent uses cached refresh token; falls back to interactive on UI redirect | P:PLAN/4.1 implement | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0262 | C/0.19/3.5] temporal-docs | A:"How does Temporal continueAsNew preserve search attributes?" | B:explanation: search attributes carried explicitly via WithSearchAttributes option | P:PLAN/4.1 use | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0263 | C/0.21/3.3] openapi-3.1+swagger-codegen | A:"How does Swagger Codegen generate enum types?" | B:explanation: enum schema → typed enum class with stringValueOf accessor | P:PLAN/4.5 configure | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0264 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519+spring-security-docs | A:"How does Spring Security validate JWT bearer in resource server?" | B:explanation: jwtAuthenticationConverter applies, JwtDecoder verifies signature against issuer JWKS | P:BUG/4.1 fix | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0265 | C/0.22/3.5] dapr-docs | A:"How does Dapr's actor placement service distribute actors across hosts?" | B:explanation: consistent-hashing keyed by actor ID; placement service maintains lookup table | P:PLAN/4.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

using the wrong-domain action vocabulary.

[CODEQ-0840 | C/0.20/3.5] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does Kubernetes Pod priority differ from preemption?" | B:explanation: priority = numeric value; preemption = high-priority pod preempts lower-priority on full node | P:PLAN/4.3 understand | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-0841 | C/0.22/3.5] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Step Functions Express vs Standard differ?" | B:explanation: Express = high-throughput, sub-second; Standard = long-running, exactly-once, audit-trail | P:PLAN/4.5 select | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0842 | C/0.19/3.5] gcp-docs | A:"How does Google Cloud Spanner secondary index differ from interleaved index?" | B:explanation: secondary = separate B-tree; interleaved = co-located with primary key (avoids cross-shard query) | P:PLAN/4.5 use | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0843 | C/0.21/3.5] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Cosmos DB partition key choice affect performance?" | B:explanation: high cardinality + frequent values = balanced load; low cardinality = hot partition | P:PLAN/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0844 | C/0.20/3.5] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does Kubernetes ServiceAccount differ from User?" | B:explanation: ServiceAccount = pod identity; User = human identity (managed externally — IDP) | P:PLAN/4.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0845 | C/0.22/3.5] envoy-docs | A:"How does Envoy admin endpoint expose runtime configuration?" | B:explanation: /config_dump shows full configuration; /clusters lists upstreams; /listeners lists L3/L4 | P:PLAN/4.3 use | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0846 | C/0.19/3.5] istio-docs | A:"How does Istio TrafficGraph differ from Kiali?" | B:explanation: TrafficGraph = aggregated topology; Kiali = service-mesh visualizer + observability | P:PLAN/4.5 select | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0847 | C/0.21/3.5] terraform-docs | A:"How does Terraform's drift detection work?" | B:explanation: terraform plan compares state to actual infrastructure; drift = real differs from state | P:PLAN/4.3 use | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0848 | C/0.20/3.5] vault-docs | A:"How does Vault Transit Engine differ from KMS?" | B:explanation: Transit = key-as-a-service (encrypt/decrypt) without storing data; KMS = manages keys for storage encryption | P:PLAN/4.5 select | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0849 | C/0.22/3.5] consul-docs | A:"How does Consul Connect mTLS work?" | B:explanation: each service has cert + identity; sidecar proxy terminates/originates mTLS based on intentions | P:PLAN/4.3 enable | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0850 | C/0.19/3.5] kafka-docs | A:"How does Kafka Connect handle offset management?" | B:explanation: offsets stored in __consumer_offsets topic; per-task; can be reset via REST API | P:PLAN/4.5 use | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §7 — More database internals (rows 851-880)

[CODEQ-0851 | C/0.20/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres MVCC handle long-running transactions?" | B:explanation: row versions retained until oldest snapshot ends; bloat grows; vacuum cannot reclaim until tx ends | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question, not contract design]
[CODEQ-0852 | C/0.18/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres index-only scan differ from index scan?" | B:explanation: index-only avoids heap access when all needed cols are in index + visibility map says page all-visible | P:ARCH/3.4 strategy | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0853 | C/0.21/3.2] mysql-docs | A:"How does MySQL InnoDB undo log support MVCC?" | B:explanation: undo records previous row versions for read view; rollback uses same; pruned by purge thread | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0854 | C/0.19/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does HOT update differ from regular update?" | B:explanation: HOT keeps tuple on same page when no indexed cols change; index entry untouched | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0855 | C/0.22/3.2] cassandra-docs | A:"How does Cassandra LWT use Paxos?" | B:explanation: lightweight tx uses 4-round Paxos for compare-and-set within partition; no cross-partition support | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0856 | C/0.20/3.2] mongo-docs | A:"How does MongoDB's WiredTiger snapshot work?" | B:explanation: per-tx snapshot via timestamp; readers see consistent point in time without locks | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0857 | C/0.18/3.2] redis-docs | A:"How does Redis sorted set ZADD GT option behave?" | B:explanation: GT = update only if new score > existing; with NX or XX flags also applicable | P:PLAN/4.5 use | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0858 | C/0.21/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres BRIN index differ from B-tree?" | B:explanation: BRIN stores per-block ranges; small + lossy; great for naturally ordered columns | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0859 | C/0.19/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres trigger ordering work for multiple triggers?" | B:explanation: alphabetical by name within event-table; AFTER triggers fire end-of-statement | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0860 | C/0.22/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres logical replication slot prevent WAL pruning?" | B:explanation: slot retains WAL until consumer acknowledges; abandoned slot causes unbounded WAL growth | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0861 | C/0.20/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres LISTEN/NOTIFY differ from triggers + queue?" | B:explanation: in-process pub/sub; messages volatile (lost on disconnect); not durable like queue | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0862 | C/0.18/3.2] mongo-docs | A:"How does MongoDB sharding choose chunk migration target?" | B:explanation: balancer picks chunks from largest shard; moves to least-loaded; per-collection threshold | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0863 | C/0.21/3.2] elastic-docs | A:"How does Elasticsearch translog flush work?" | B:explanation: writes go to translog + memory buffer; flush converts to segment + clears translog | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0864 | C/0.19/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres EXPLAIN ANALYZE differ from EXPLAIN?" | B:explanation: ANALYZE actually runs query + reports actual times; EXPLAIN shows estimated plan only | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0865 | C/0.22/3.2] cassandra-docs | A:"How does Cassandra hinted handoff work?" | B:explanation: coordinator stores hint when replica down; replays when up; bounded retention | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0866 | C/0.20/3.2] redis-docs | A:"How does Redis cluster slot migration work?" | B:explanation: per-slot migration via MIGRATE; reads served from source until handoff | P:ARCH/3.4 strategy | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0867 | C/0.18/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres SET search_path affect schema resolution?" | B:explanation: search_path = ordered list of schemas; first match wins; pg_catalog implicitly first | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0868 | C/0.21/3.2] mysql-docs | A:"How does MySQL group replication conflict detection work?" | B:explanation: each tx writes certification info; replicas vote; conflicts rejected at commit | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0869 | C/0.19/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres prepared statement plan caching work?" | B:explanation: plan cached after 5 executions; chooses generic vs custom by cost heuristic | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0870 | C/0.22/3.2] mongo-docs | A:"How does MongoDB change stream resumability work?" | B:explanation: each event has resume token; pass token on next stream open to continue from gap | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0871 | C/0.20/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres unlogged table differ from temp?" | B:explanation: unlogged = no WAL (faster, lost on crash); temp = session-scoped, also no WAL | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0872 | C/0.18/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres parallel query split work load?" | B:explanation: leader spawns workers; workers process partitions; leader gathers + finalizes | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0873 | C/0.21/3.2] mysql-docs | A:"How does MySQL binlog format ROW differ from STATEMENT?" | B:explanation: ROW logs actual row changes; STATEMENT logs SQL text; ROW safer for non-deterministic queries | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0874 | C/0.19/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres exclusion constraint differ from unique?" | B:explanation: exclusion checks operator (e.g., overlap) across rows; unique checks equality only | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0875 | C/0.22/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres advisory lock scope work?" | B:explanation: pg_advisory_lock = session-scoped; pg_advisory_xact_lock = tx-scoped | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0876 | C/0.20/3.2] redis-docs | A:"How does Redis stream XADD differ from LPUSH?" | B:explanation: stream = append-only log with consumer groups + acks; list = simple LPUSH/RPOP | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0877 | C/0.18/3.2] elastic-docs | A:"How does Elasticsearch mapping change strategy work?" | B:explanation: existing fields immutable; new fields can be added; type changes need reindex | P:ARCH/3.4 strategy | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0878 | C/0.21/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres role membership inheritance work?" | B:explanation: NOINHERIT = explicit SET ROLE needed; INHERIT (default) = privileges flow automatically | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0879 | C/0.19/3.2] postgres-docs | A:"How does Postgres COPY FREEZE optimize bulk load?" | B:explanation: marks rows as frozen at load time, skipping later vacuum freeze; requires same-tx CREATE | P:ARCH/3.5 monitor | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0880 | C/0.22/3.2] mysql-docs | A:"How does MySQL ALGORITHM=INSTANT online DDL work?" | B:explanation: metadata-only changes (e.g., add column at end); no data copy or rebuild | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §8 — More security internals (rows 881-910)

[CODEQ-0881 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does JWT exp claim differ from nbf?" | B:explanation: exp = not-after timestamp; nbf = not-before timestamp; both in NumericDate seconds | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0882 | C/0.18/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does HTTP Message Signature signature-input header work?" | B:explanation: lists which components sign; ordered; with sig label binding to signature header | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0883 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP proof bind to access token?" | B:explanation: jkt thumbprint embedded in token; client provides DPoP proof using same key on each call | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0884 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-7591 | A:"How does dynamic-client-registration return client_id and secret?" | B:explanation: 201 Created with JSON containing client_id, client_secret, registration_access_token | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0885 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-7636 | A:"How does PKCE protect against authorization-code interception?" | B:explanation: client sends code_challenge with auth req; verifies code_verifier on token req; binding | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0886 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-8725 | A:"How does JWT BCP recommend handling alg field?" | B:explanation: explicit algorithm allowlist by application; never accept 'none'; verify per registered key | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0887 | C/0.18/3.4] csp-l3 | A:"How does CSP nonce differ from hash?" | B:explanation: nonce = random per-response; hash = static digest of script content; both whitelist mechanisms | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0888 | C/0.21/3.4] cwe-352 | A:"How does double-submit-cookie pattern protect against CSRF?" | B:explanation: server compares value in cookie to value in header/form; cross-origin cannot read cookie | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0889 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-9457 | A:"How does problem detail extension fields work?" | B:explanation: any extra members allowed; consumers ignore unknown; type URI dereferenceable | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0890 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-9116 | A:"How does security.txt Expires field interact with consumers?" | B:explanation: Expires required; consumers ignore expired entries; cadence forces maintenance | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0891 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7515 | A:"How does JWS detached signature differ from compact serialization?" | B:explanation: detached = signature separate from payload; compact = base64url payload + sig in same string | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0892 | C/0.18/3.4] cwe-79 | A:"How does HTML attribute encoding differ from element encoding?" | B:explanation: attribute = encode all non-alpha-numeric per WHATWG HTML serialisation rules; element = HTMLEntity | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0893 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does JWT unprotected header differ from protected?" | B:explanation: protected = signed; unprotected = transferable but not signed; flat-JSON serialization only | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0894 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-9201 | A:"How does RFC 9201 client credentials differ from RFC 8252?" | B:explanation: 9201 expands client metadata for native; 8252 covers BCP for native flows | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0895 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP nonce mitigate replay?" | B:explanation: server can challenge with use-dpop-nonce; client includes in next proof; window-binds | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0896 | C/0.20/3.4] cwe-89 | A:"How does parameterised query prevent SQL injection?" | B:explanation: parameters bound separately; never concatenated; database treats as values not SQL | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0897 | C/0.18/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does HTTP Message Signature created vs expires parameters work?" | B:explanation: created = signing time; expires = validity expiry; both prevent replay | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0898 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does JWT iss claim canonicalisation work?" | B:explanation: case-sensitive string; no canonical form; verifier compares exact match | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0899 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-7591 | A:"How does software_statement differ from software_id?" | B:explanation: statement = signed JWT containing metadata; id = identifier alone | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0900 | C/0.22/3.4] csp-l3 | A:"How does CSP strict-dynamic enable trusted-script propagation?" | B:explanation: nonces propagate to dynamically-created scripts; allowlist-domains ignored | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0901 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-7591 | A:"How does redirect_uri verification work in OAuth?" | B:explanation: server enforces exact-match against registered uris; partial-match disallowed | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0902 | C/0.18/3.4] rfc-9239 | A:"How does ECMAScript media type registration handle versioning?" | B:explanation: parameters indicate version; default to latest stable when omitted | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0903 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-9116 | A:"How does security.txt PGP signing work in practice?" | B:explanation: file signed with PGP; signature inline or detached; consumers verify against published key | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0904 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-7515 | A:"How does JWS critical (crit) header parameter work?" | B:explanation: lists header params recipient must understand; reject if unrecognised | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0905 | C/0.22/3.4] cwe-22 | A:"How does path canonicalisation prevent traversal?" | B:explanation: resolve to absolute path; check against allowed prefix; reject .. or symlinks | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0906 | C/0.20/3.4] cwe-327 | A:"How does AES-GCM differ from AES-CBC for AEAD?" | B:explanation: GCM = single pass authenticated encryption; CBC needs separate HMAC | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0907 | C/0.18/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does JWT sub claim canonicalisation work for case?" | B:explanation: case-sensitive; treat as opaque string; comparator must use exact match | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0908 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does HTTP Message Signature key-id resolution work?" | B:explanation: keyid parameter identifies signing key; resolver fetches public via JWKS or static map | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0909 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-9457 | A:"How does problem detail handle multiple errors in single response?" | B:explanation: errors array as extension; each entry full problem detail; outer instance summary | P:ARCH/3.3 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0910 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP htm parameter work?" | B:explanation: htm = HTTP method binding; htu = URL binding; both prevent token reuse on different endpoint | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §9 — More observability + tracing (rows 911-940)

[CODEQ-0911 | C/0.20/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel parent-based sampling propagate decisions?" | B:explanation: child inherits parent sampled flag via traceparent; sampler can override at root | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0912 | C/0.18/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel resource detection work for k8s?" | B:explanation: k8s detector reads pod metadata via downward API; sets k8s.* resource attributes | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0913 | C/0.21/3.5] otel-semconv | A:"How does OTel semconv handle versioning?" | B:explanation: schema_url field per resource; library publishes semconv version it conforms to | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0914 | C/0.19/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel batching exporter handle overload?" | B:explanation: bounded queue + sized batches; drop oldest on overflow; warn when drop rate high | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0915 | C/0.22/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel logs API differ from existing logging libraries?" | B:explanation: bridges existing logger via appender; attaches trace-context to log records | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0916 | C/0.20/3.5] prometheus-docs | A:"How does Prometheus rate vs irate differ?" | B:explanation: rate = average over window; irate = instantaneous (last 2 samples) | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0917 | C/0.18/3.5] prometheus-docs | A:"How does Prometheus recording rule differ from alert rule?" | B:explanation: recording = derived series stored; alert = raises alert on expr true for duration | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0918 | C/0.21/3.5] grafana-docs | A:"How does Grafana threshold alert on transformation work?" | B:explanation: data source returns series; transformer reduces to single value; threshold applied to value | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0919 | C/0.19/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel context propagation across goroutines work?" | B:explanation: explicit context.WithValue + pass to child; auto-instrumentation may not span goroutine boundary | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0920 | C/0.22/3.5] datadog-docs | A:"How does Datadog APM service map differ from trace search?" | B:explanation: map = topology of services; search = querying individual traces | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0921 | C/0.20/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel span events differ from logs?" | B:explanation: events = part of span (attributed); logs = independent records linked via trace_id | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0922 | C/0.18/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel link differ from parent-child relation?" | B:explanation: parent-child = call hierarchy; link = causal but not nested (e.g., batch processing) | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0923 | C/0.21/3.5] prometheus-docs | A:"How does Prometheus federation differ from remote-write?" | B:explanation: federation = pull from another Prometheus; remote-write = push to long-term store | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0924 | C/0.19/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel exemplar in metric work?" | B:explanation: metric data point optional exemplar with trace_id; links series to specific trace | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0925 | C/0.22/3.5] grafana-docs | A:"How does Grafana Loki LogQL differ from Elasticsearch DSL?" | B:explanation: LogQL = label-based stream selectors + line filters; ES DSL = inverted-index over content | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0926 | C/0.20/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel histogram bucket selection work?" | B:explanation: explicit boundaries or exponential; default OTel histogram exponential w/ 160 buckets | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0927 | C/0.18/3.5] datadog-docs | A:"How does Datadog log pipeline processor work?" | B:explanation: ordered processors transform/route logs; grok extracts; remapper renames | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0928 | C/0.21/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel collector pipeline differ from agent?" | B:explanation: collector = standalone process with receivers/processors/exporters; agent = sidecar | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0929 | C/0.19/3.5] prometheus-docs | A:"How does Prometheus relabel_config order work?" | B:explanation: stages applied top-down; drop relabels remove before subsequent stages run | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0930 | C/0.22/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel context detach work in async code?" | B:explanation: detach removes context from current scope; manual attach restores at await boundary | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0931 | C/0.20/3.5] grafana-docs | A:"How does Grafana annotation differ from alert?" | B:explanation: annotation = mark on dashboard at timestamp; alert = active rule with state | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0932 | C/0.18/3.5] otel-semconv | A:"How does OTel semconv recommend HTTP server route attribute?" | B:explanation: http.route = route template (e.g., /users/:id); avoids high-cardinality URL value | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0933 | C/0.21/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel span status differ from HTTP status code attribute?" | B:explanation: span status = OTel-defined OK/Error/Unset; http.status_code = wire status | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0934 | C/0.19/3.5] datadog-docs | A:"How does Datadog forwarder differ from agent?" | B:explanation: forwarder = serverless lambda for cloudwatch logs; agent = host-based collector | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0935 | C/0.22/3.5] prometheus-docs | A:"How does Prometheus alert grouping work in alertmanager?" | B:explanation: group_by labels collect alerts into single notification; group_wait + group_interval timing | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0936 | C/0.20/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel tail-based sampling differ from head-based?" | B:explanation: head = decide at root span; tail = decide after seeing entire trace; tail catches errors | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0937 | C/0.18/3.5] grafana-docs | A:"How does Grafana variable refresh trigger work?" | B:explanation: refresh = on dashboard load or on time-range change; query reruns on trigger | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0938 | C/0.21/3.5] otel-semconv | A:"How does OTel semconv handle DB system attribute?" | B:explanation: db.system = enumerated string (e.g., postgresql, mysql, redis); aids cross-DB analysis | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0939 | C/0.19/3.5] otel-spec | A:"How does OTel resource attribute precedence work?" | B:explanation: explicit > env > detector > SDK default; documented in spec | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0940 | C/0.22/3.5] prometheus-docs | A:"How does Prometheus pushgateway differ from scrape?" | B:explanation: pushgateway = receives metrics from short-lived jobs; scrape = pull from long-running | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §10 — More framework + library internals (rows 941-1000)

[CODEQ-0941 | C/0.20/3.4] dotnet-docs | A:"How does ASP.NET Core middleware execution order work?" | B:explanation: top-down on request; bottom-up on response; order in Program.cs determines pipeline | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0942 | C/0.18/3.4] dotnet-docs | A:"How does .NET ConfigureAwait(false) affect SynchronizationContext?" | B:explanation: false = don't capture context; allows continuation on thread pool; avoids deadlock | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0943 | C/0.21/3.4] spring-docs | A:"How does Spring's @Transactional propagation REQUIRES_NEW work?" | B:explanation: suspends current tx; opens new tx; commits independently; resumes outer on return | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0944 | C/0.19/3.4] react-docs | A:"How does React useEffect dependency array work?" | B:explanation: comparison reference equality; runs effect on mount + on any dep change | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0945 | C/0.22/3.4] vue-docs | A:"How does Vue reactivity proxy differ from defineProperty?" | B:explanation: proxy = transparent intercept of all property access; defineProperty = per-prop config | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0946 | C/0.20/3.4] svelte-docs | A:"How does Svelte $: reactive declaration work at compile time?" | B:explanation: compiler generates dependency-tracking code; runs block when any var on RHS changes | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0947 | C/0.18/3.4] angular-docs | A:"How does Angular zone.js differ from manual change detection?" | B:explanation: zone.js patches async APIs; auto-triggers change detection; can be disabled per-component | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0948 | C/0.21/3.4] tailwind-docs | A:"How does Tailwind JIT mode differ from AOT?" | B:explanation: JIT = generates classes on demand from template scan; AOT = pre-generated full library | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0949 | C/0.19/3.4] redux-docs | A:"How does Redux Toolkit createSlice differ from raw reducer + actions?" | B:explanation: createSlice generates action creators + reducer; uses Immer for immutable updates | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0950 | C/0.22/3.4] graphql-docs | A:"How does GraphQL DataLoader prevent N+1?" | B:explanation: batches load calls within a tick; caches per-request; reduces DB calls | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0951 | C/0.20/3.4] apollo-docs | A:"How does Apollo Client cache normalization work?" | B:explanation: each entity stored by __typename + id; queries reference entities; updates propagate | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0952 | C/0.18/3.4] auth0-docs | A:"How does Auth0 silent authentication work?" | B:explanation: hidden iframe to /authorize with prompt=none; gets new token without user interaction | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0953 | C/0.21/3.4] firebase-docs | A:"How does Firebase Auth ID token verification work?" | B:explanation: verify signature with Firebase public keys; check iss + aud + exp + auth_time | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0954 | C/0.19/3.4] supabase-docs | A:"How does Supabase row-level security work?" | B:explanation: postgres RLS policies; each row checked against current user via JWT claims | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0955 | C/0.22/3.4] postgrest-docs | A:"How does PostgREST role switching per request work?" | B:explanation: derives role from JWT claim; SET LOCAL ROLE per request; RLS applies | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0956 | C/0.20/3.4] hasura-docs | A:"How does Hasura permission rule differ from postgres RLS?" | B:explanation: Hasura permissions enforce at GraphQL layer before SQL; RLS checks at row level | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0957 | C/0.18/3.4] dapr-docs | A:"How does Dapr state-store concurrency option work?" | B:explanation: first-write-wins or last-write-wins; eTag-based optimistic concurrency on update | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0958 | C/0.21/3.4] temporal-docs | A:"How does Temporal child workflow differ from activity?" | B:explanation: child workflow = recursive workflow with own history; activity = single non-deterministic op | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0959 | C/0.19/3.4] knative-docs | A:"How does Knative Serving scale-to-zero work?" | B:explanation: Activator buffers requests when scaled to 0; triggers cold start; routes via Kourier | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0960 | C/0.22/3.4] argo-docs | A:"How does Argo Rollouts canary differ from blue-green?" | B:explanation: canary = progressive traffic shift via experiment steps; BG = atomic switch with previous kept | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0961 | C/0.20/3.4] flux-docs | A:"How does Flux v2 differ from v1 in reconciliation?" | B:explanation: v2 = source/kustomize/helm controllers; v1 = single Flux pod; v2 GitOps Toolkit composable | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0962 | C/0.18/3.4] kustomize-docs | A:"How does Kustomize patch strategic merge work?" | B:explanation: matches resources by GVK + name + namespace; merges patch fields by k8s patch strategy | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0963 | C/0.21/3.4] helm-docs | A:"How does Helm 3 secret-storage differ from Helm 2 ConfigMap?" | B:explanation: v3 secrets in releases by default; v2 used ConfigMaps in tiller namespace | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0964 | C/0.19/3.4] vault-docs | A:"How does Vault dynamic database secret work?" | B:explanation: Vault generates per-app DB user with TTL; auto-revokes on lease end; rotates seamlessly | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0965 | C/0.22/3.4] consul-docs | A:"How does Consul intentions enforce service-to-service auth?" | B:explanation: intent is allow/deny rule between source-dest; enforced by sidecar proxy at L4 mTLS | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0966 | C/0.20/3.4] envoy-docs | A:"How does Envoy circuit breaker differ from Hystrix?" | B:explanation: Envoy = connection-pool + outlier-detection in proxy; Hystrix = library code-level breaker | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0967 | C/0.18/3.4] istio-docs | A:"How does Istio destination rule connectionPool work?" | B:explanation: per-host TCP/HTTP connection limits in sidecar; refuses excess via 503 or queue | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0968 | C/0.21/3.4] linkerd-docs | A:"How does Linkerd proxy retry logic work?" | B:explanation: retry budgeted (max 20% extra reqs); only retries on idempotent or marked-safe routes | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0969 | C/0.19/3.4] traefik-docs | A:"How does Traefik dynamic config differ from static?" | B:explanation: static = startup options (entrypoints, providers); dynamic = routers/services updated live | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0970 | C/0.22/3.4] nginx-docs | A:"How does Nginx upstream keepalive directive work?" | B:explanation: caches idle connections to upstream; reuses for HTTP/1.1; reduces handshake overhead | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0971 | C/0.20/3.4] github-actions-docs | A:"How does GitHub Actions matrix expansion work?" | B:explanation: cartesian product over keys with array values; include/exclude refine; per-combo job | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0972 | C/0.18/3.4] gitlab-ci-docs | A:"How does GitLab CI rules differ from only/except?" | B:explanation: rules = newer, supports if/changes/exists/when; only/except = legacy, less expressive | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0973 | C/0.21/3.4] sonarqube-docs | A:"How does SonarQube quality gate condition work?" | B:explanation: each condition checks metric against threshold; gate fails if any condition fails on PR | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0974 | C/0.19/3.4] terraform-docs | A:"How does Terraform plugin protocol differ between v5 and v6?" | B:explanation: v5 = legacy protobuf; v6 = enhanced (fwk type system, write-only attrs) | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0975 | C/0.22/3.4] etcd-docs | A:"How does etcd Watch differ from polling?" | B:explanation: Watch = server pushes notifications on key change; polling = periodic Read + diff | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0976 | C/0.20/3.4] kafka-docs | A:"How does Kafka log retention differ from compaction?" | B:explanation: retention = time/size based pruning; compaction = keeps latest value per key | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0977 | C/0.18/3.4] cncf-docs | A:"How does CNCF graduation differ from incubation?" | B:explanation: incubation = active community + production users; graduation = mature governance + adopters | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0978 | C/0.21/3.4] opa-docs | A:"How does OPA Rego data versus input differ?" | B:explanation: input = per-query payload from client; data = global policy state pushed via API | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0979 | C/0.19/3.4] oauth-2.1-draft | A:"How does OAuth 2.1 differ from OAuth 2.0?" | B:explanation: 2.1 mandates PKCE for all flows + drops implicit + drops password grant + tightens redirect | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0980 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does RFC 9421 derived component differ from header?" | B:explanation: derived = computed property like @method or @target-uri; header = HTTP header value | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-0981 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP key-rotation work in long sessions?" | B:explanation: client must re-issue DPoP-bound token with new key; server requires re-auth on key change | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-0985 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-9201 | A:"How does RFC 9201 software_id work for native clients?" | B:explanation: identifies app across installations; usually static identifier published with the app | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1001 | C/0.20/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Lambda provisioned concurrency differ from on-demand?" | B:explanation: provisioned = pre-warmed instances + reserved concurrency; on-demand = scales from 0 | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1004 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Service Bus session enable ordered processing?" | B:explanation: messages with same SessionId locked to single receiver; FIFO within session | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1005 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS DynamoDB DAX differ from ElastiCache?" | B:explanation: DAX = write-through cache for DynamoDB API; ElastiCache = generic Redis/Memcached | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1006 | C/0.20/3.4] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s ServiceMesh differ from CNI plugin?" | B:explanation: mesh = L7 traffic management with sidecars; CNI = L3/L4 pod networking plumbing | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1007 | C/0.18/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS API Gateway REST differ from HTTP API?" | B:explanation: REST = full features (caching, request/response transform); HTTP = simpler + cheaper | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1008 | C/0.21/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS CloudFront origin-request policy differ from cache-key policy?" | B:explanation: origin-request = headers/qs sent to origin; cache-key = elements forming cache identity | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1009 | C/0.19/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Pub/Sub message ordering work?" | B:explanation: enable orderingKey on publish; subscribers read ordered per key; throughput per-key cap | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1010 | C/0.22/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Cosmos DB request units differ from throughput provisioning?" | B:explanation: RU = normalized cost per op; throughput = RU/sec budget per container or db | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1011 | C/0.20/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS EventBridge schema differ from SNS topic?" | B:explanation: EventBridge = schema-aware bus + replay + cross-account; SNS = simple pub/sub fanout | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1012 | C/0.18/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS WAF rule group differ from web ACL?" | B:explanation: rule group = reusable rule set; web ACL = scoped policy attaching rule groups + rules | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1013 | C/0.21/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP IAM Conditions differ from custom roles?" | B:explanation: conditions = attribute-based predicates on bindings; custom roles = redefine perm set | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1014 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Front Door differ from Application Gateway?" | B:explanation: Front Door = global edge with anycast; App Gateway = regional layer-7 LB | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1015 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Aurora I/O optimized cluster differ from standard?" | B:explanation: I/O opt = no per-IO charge, higher base; standard = pay per read/write IO | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1016 | C/0.20/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Step Functions intrinsic function work?" | B:explanation: built-in functions like States.Format / States.JsonToString; usable in input/output paths | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1017 | C/0.18/3.4] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s ResourceQuota scope-selector work?" | B:explanation: scope-selector matches by PriorityClass or BestEffort etc; quota applies to matching pods | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1018 | C/0.21/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS S3 Object Lock differ from versioning?" | B:explanation: Object Lock = WORM with retention period; versioning = preserves prior versions | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1019 | C/0.19/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP VPC Service Controls differ from firewall?" | B:explanation: VPC SC = perimeter for managed services (BigQuery etc); firewall = VM network rules | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1020 | C/0.22/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Private Link differ from VNet Peering?" | B:explanation: Private Link = endpoint to PaaS via private IP; Peering = full VNet-to-VNet connect | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1021 | C/0.20/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Lambda destinations differ from DLQ?" | B:explanation: destinations = success + failure routing to SNS/SQS/Lambda; DLQ = failure-only to SQS/SNS | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1022 | C/0.18/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Backup vault lock differ from S3 Object Lock?" | B:explanation: vault lock = WORM on AWS Backup recovery points; S3 lock = WORM on objects | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1023 | C/0.21/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Workload Identity Federation differ from service account key?" | B:explanation: federation = exchange external IdP token; key = static credentials (avoid) | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1024 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Bicep differ from ARM JSON?" | B:explanation: Bicep = DSL compiling to ARM JSON; less verbose; modules + parameters first-class | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1025 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS RDS Proxy differ from PgBouncer?" | B:explanation: Proxy = managed pool with IAM auth + secrets manager; PgBouncer = self-host generic | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1026 | C/0.20/3.4] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s VolumeSnapshotClass differ from StorageClass?" | B:explanation: VolumeSnapshotClass = snapshot driver + parameters; StorageClass = volume provisioner | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1027 | C/0.18/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS CloudWatch Composite Alarm differ from regular?" | B:explanation: composite = boolean expression over alarm states; regular = single metric threshold | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1028 | C/0.21/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Anthos Service Mesh differ from open-source Istio?" | B:explanation: Anthos = managed control plane + Google support; OSS = self-managed | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1029 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Container Apps differ from AKS?" | B:explanation: Container Apps = serverless containers + KEDA; AKS = full k8s control | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1030 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Config conformance pack differ from individual rules?" | B:explanation: conformance pack = grouped + parameterised set of rules; deploys atomically | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
