[CODEQ-1033 | C/0.21/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Cloud Build cloud-builders pattern work?" | B:explanation: each step pulls a cloud-builder image; runs command; pipeline serializes by default | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1034 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Functions Premium plan differ from Consumption?" | B:explanation: Premium = pre-warmed + VNet + unlimited duration; Consumption = scale-to-zero, 10 min cap | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1035 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Step Functions Map state differ from Parallel?" | B:explanation: Map = iterate over array with one branch; Parallel = fixed branches running in parallel | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1036 | C/0.20/3.4] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s downward API differ from env from configmap?" | B:explanation: downward = pod metadata (name, ip); env from cm = explicit values | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1037 | C/0.18/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS X-Ray sampling rules work?" | B:explanation: rules match service/route; reservoir size + rate; first-match wins | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1038 | C/0.21/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Cloud Trace sampling rule work?" | B:explanation: per-service sampling rate; can override via traceparent header sampled-flag | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1039 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Application Insights ingestion sampling differ from adaptive?" | B:explanation: ingestion = sample at SDK before send; adaptive = adjusts rate dynamically | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1040 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Glue crawler infer schema?" | B:explanation: scans first ~1MB or all-records; infers types; creates/updates tables in Glue Data Catalog | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1041 | C/0.20/3.4] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s vertical pod autoscaler differ from horizontal?" | B:explanation: VPA = adjusts requests/limits; HPA = adjusts replica count | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1042 | C/0.18/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS PrivateLink differ from VPC Peering?" | B:explanation: PrivateLink = endpoint to specific service; Peering = full VPC-to-VPC route | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1043 | C/0.21/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Filestore differ from Cloud Storage?" | B:explanation: Filestore = managed NFS file system for VMs; Cloud Storage = object storage | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1044 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure ExpressRoute differ from VPN Gateway?" | B:explanation: ExpressRoute = dedicated private circuit; VPN Gateway = encrypted over public internet | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1045 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Step Functions Express vs Standard pricing differ?" | B:explanation: Express = pay per invocation + duration (high-vol); Standard = per state transition | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1046 | C/0.20/3.4] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s topology aware hints work?" | B:explanation: services prefer endpoints in same zone; hints set by EndpointSlice controller | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1047 | C/0.18/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Lambda Layer differ from container image?" | B:explanation: layer = up to 5 zip overlays on Lambda runtime; image = full container with own runtime | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1048 | C/0.21/3.4] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Memorystore Redis differ from self-host on GKE?" | B:explanation: managed = patching/HA/snapshot included; GKE = full control + ops burden | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1049 | C/0.19/3.4] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure DevOps pipeline YAML differ from classic UI?" | B:explanation: YAML = source-controlled, multi-stage; classic UI = visual editor, single stage | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1050 | C/0.22/3.4] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS WAF managed rule group differ from Marketplace rule group?" | B:explanation: managed = AWS-maintained free/paid; Marketplace = third-party priced | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §12 — Design pattern explanations (rows 1051-1090)

[CODEQ-1051 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does the Strategy pattern differ from State pattern?" | B:explanation: Strategy = pluggable algorithm chosen by client; State = transitions internally per state machine | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1052 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Decorator differ from Proxy?" | B:explanation: Decorator adds behaviour transparently; Proxy controls access (lazy/remote/protection) | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1053 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Adapter differ from Bridge?" | B:explanation: Adapter unifies incompatible interfaces; Bridge decouples abstraction from implementation | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1054 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Composite differ from Decorator structurally?" | B:explanation: Composite = uniform tree of leaves and nodes; Decorator = one wrapper per call | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1055 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Factory Method differ from Abstract Factory?" | B:explanation: Factory Method = single product creation polymorphism; Abstract Factory = product family creation | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1056 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Builder differ from Factory for complex DTO construction?" | B:explanation: Builder = step-by-step + variants; Factory = one-shot + selection | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1057 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Observer differ from Mediator?" | B:explanation: Observer = pub/sub one-to-many; Mediator = central hub many-to-many with logic | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1058 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Chain of Responsibility differ from Pipeline pattern?" | B:explanation: CoR = early-exit on first match; Pipeline = sequential transforms with no early-exit | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1059 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Visitor pattern enable double-dispatch?" | B:explanation: each element accept(visitor) calls visitor.visitX(this) — runtime dispatch on both | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1060 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Memento differ from snapshot in event-sourcing?" | B:explanation: Memento = encapsulated capture of object state; ES snapshot = aggregate state at event-N | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1061 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Template Method differ from Strategy?" | B:explanation: Template Method = inheritance + override hooks; Strategy = composition + interface | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1062 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Command differ from Function-as-First-Class?" | B:explanation: Command = object encapsulating call + state + undo; Function = callable only | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1063 | C/0.21/3.1] ddd-blue-book | A:"How does Aggregate differ from Entity in DDD?" | B:explanation: Aggregate = consistency boundary with root entity; Entity = identity-bearing object | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1064 | C/0.19/3.1] ddd-blue-book | A:"How does Value Object differ from Entity?" | B:explanation: VO = identity-less, equality-by-value, immutable; Entity = identity-bearing | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1065 | C/0.22/3.1] ddd-blue-book | A:"How does Domain Service differ from Application Service?" | B:explanation: Domain Service = pure domain behaviour across aggregates; App Service = use-case orchestration | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1066 | C/0.20/3.1] ddd-blue-book | A:"How does Repository pattern differ from DAO?" | B:explanation: Repository = collection-like aggregate access; DAO = per-entity CRUD abstraction | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1067 | C/0.18/3.1] ddd-blue-book | A:"How does Specification pattern compose query rules?" | B:explanation: AND/OR/NOT operators combine specs; each spec implements isSatisfiedBy + toQuery | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1068 | C/0.21/3.1] cqrs | A:"How does CQRS read-model lag interact with consumer offset?" | B:explanation: lag = events not yet projected; offset tracks consumer position; backpressure on lag | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1069 | C/0.19/3.1] saga | A:"How does choreographed Saga differ from orchestrated?" | B:explanation: choreographed = each step listens + reacts; orchestrated = central conductor | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1070 | C/0.22/3.1] event-sourcing | A:"How does event-sourcing handle event versioning over time?" | B:explanation: upcaster transforms old events to new shape on read; explicit version tag per event | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1071 | C/0.20/3.1] outbox-pattern | A:"How does transactional outbox handle relay failure?" | B:explanation: relay re-reads outbox; idempotent publish prevents duplicates | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1072 | C/0.18/3.1] hexagonal | A:"How does Hexagonal architecture enforce dependency direction?" | B:explanation: domain has zero outbound deps; ports define contracts; adapters depend on domain | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1073 | C/0.21/3.1] anti-corruption-layer | A:"How does ACL prevent legacy concepts from leaking into new context?" | B:explanation: translator maps legacy terms to new ubiquitous language at boundary | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1074 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Singleton differ from Static class with statics?" | B:explanation: Singleton = lazy init + DI-friendly; static class = early init + global access only | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1075 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Flyweight pattern enable mass small-object sharing?" | B:explanation: shared intrinsic state + extrinsic per-call state; reduces memory via sharing | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1076 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Iterator handle concurrent modification?" | B:explanation: fail-fast = throw on detected concurrent change; weakly-consistent = best-effort | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1077 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Prototype differ from copy constructor?" | B:explanation: Prototype = polymorphic clone() runtime-dispatched; copy ctor = static type | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1078 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Bridge separate abstraction from implementation?" | B:explanation: composition link from abstraction to impl interface; both vary independently | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1079 | C/0.19/3.1] ddd-blue-book | A:"How does Bounded Context differ from microservice?" | B:explanation: BC = ubiquitous-language boundary (logical); microservice = deployment unit | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1080 | C/0.22/3.1] ddd-blue-book | A:"How does Context Map differ from a system diagram?" | B:explanation: Context Map = relationship types between BCs (partner/conformist/ACL/etc) | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1081 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Null Object pattern eliminate null checks?" | B:explanation: object that does nothing safely; replaces null with no-op behaviour | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1082 | C/0.18/3.1] cqrs | A:"How does CQRS handle eventual consistency for the user?" | B:explanation: UI shows write success then polls/subscribes to read-model for confirmation | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1083 | C/0.21/3.1] saga | A:"How does Saga compensation order differ from forward order?" | B:explanation: compensations run in reverse — last successful step first | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1084 | C/0.19/3.1] event-sourcing | A:"How does event-sourcing handle GDPR right-to-erasure?" | B:explanation: crypto-shredding (delete encryption key) or selective rewrite via approved process | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1085 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Lazy Initialization Holder idiom achieve thread-safe lazy singleton?" | B:explanation: nested holder class loads on first access; JVM class-init guarantees thread-safety | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1086 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Read-Through cache pattern differ from Cache-Aside?" | B:explanation: read-through = cache fetches on miss; cache-aside = app fetches + populates | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1087 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Circuit Breaker handle half-open state?" | B:explanation: half-open admits limited probe traffic; success → closed; failure → open again | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1088 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Bulkhead pattern isolate failures?" | B:explanation: partition resources (threads/connections); failure in one bulkhead doesn't drain others | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1089 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Sidecar pattern differ from library inclusion?" | B:explanation: sidecar = separate process per pod; library = in-process; sidecar is language-agnostic | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1090 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Idempotency Key pattern handle replay safely?" | B:explanation: server stores key + result; same key returns stored result without re-processing | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §13 — UI/UX framework internals (rows 1091-1130)

[CODEQ-1091 | C/0.20/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React reconciliation choose to update vs replace?" | B:explanation: same type+key = update; different type or different key = unmount + mount | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1092 | C/0.18/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React Suspense interact with lazy() and data fetching?" | B:explanation: Suspense unwinds promises; renders fallback until promise settles; works with lazy + RSC | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1093 | C/0.21/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React useTransition differ from useDeferredValue?" | B:explanation: useTransition = mark updates as non-urgent; useDeferredValue = defer specific value | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1094 | C/0.19/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React Server Components stream HTML?" | B:explanation: RSC renders on server → streams JSX-as-payload → client merges into tree | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1095 | C/0.22/3.6] vue-docs | A:"How does Vue 3 reactivity track dependencies via Proxy?" | B:explanation: Proxy traps get/set; effect collected on get; runs on set; transparent for nested objects | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1096 | C/0.20/3.6] vue-docs | A:"How does Vue Pinia differ from Vuex 4?" | B:explanation: Pinia = composition-API native, no mutations, type-safe; Vuex = options-style + mutations | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1097 | C/0.18/3.6] svelte-docs | A:"How does Svelte compiler eliminate virtual DOM?" | B:explanation: compiler emits direct DOM updates per reactive var; no diffing at runtime | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1098 | C/0.21/3.6] angular-docs | A:"How does Angular Signals trigger view update?" | B:explanation: signal change marks consumers dirty; effect/computed re-runs on dirty + read | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1099 | C/0.19/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React StrictMode help find issues?" | B:explanation: double-invokes effects in dev to surface impure functions; deprecates legacy APIs | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1100 | C/0.22/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React.memo differ from useMemo?" | B:explanation: React.memo = component-level prop-equality skip; useMemo = value memoisation per-render | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1101 | C/0.20/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does Next.js App Router differ from Pages Router?" | B:explanation: App = server components by default + nested layouts; Pages = client + per-page only | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1102 | C/0.18/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does Next.js dynamic import differ from React.lazy?" | B:explanation: next/dynamic supports SSR control + loading state; React.lazy needs Suspense + client | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1103 | C/0.21/3.6] vue-docs | A:"How does Vue script setup differ from setup() function?" | B:explanation: script setup = compiler treats top-level bindings as exposed; setup() returns explicit object | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1104 | C/0.19/3.6] svelte-docs | A:"How does SvelteKit form action differ from API endpoint?" | B:explanation: form action = +page.server.ts actions; runs on POST; API endpoint = +server.ts | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1105 | C/0.22/3.6] angular-docs | A:"How does Angular standalone component differ from NgModule?" | B:explanation: standalone = imports its own deps; NgModule = central registration; standalone is preferred | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1106 | C/0.20/3.6] tailwind-docs | A:"How does Tailwind JIT compile classes per template scan?" | B:explanation: JIT scans content paths; emits CSS for found classes; arbitrary values supported | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1107 | C/0.18/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS container queries differ from media queries?" | B:explanation: container = parent size based; media = viewport based; CQ enables component-local responsiveness | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1108 | C/0.21/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS cascade layers (@layer) order rules?" | B:explanation: lower-priority layers first; explicit ordering overrides specificity within layers | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1109 | C/0.19/3.6] aria-spec | A:"How does ARIA aria-current differ from aria-selected?" | B:explanation: aria-current = current item in set (page/step); aria-selected = listbox/tab selection | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1110 | C/0.22/3.6] aria-spec | A:"How does role=alert differ from aria-live=assertive?" | B:explanation: role=alert is shorthand for aria-live=assertive + atomic; both interrupt SR queue | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1111 | C/0.20/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 'target size minimum' work?" | B:explanation: 24×24 CSS px target size with exceptions; equivalent path or inline | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1112 | C/0.18/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 1.4.3 'contrast minimum' apply to icons?" | B:explanation: applies to text + meaningful graphical-objects; aesthetic icons exempt | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1113 | C/0.21/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.5.7 'dragging movements' work?" | B:explanation: drag-essential interactions need single-pointer alternative (e.g., click-step) | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1114 | C/0.19/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.4.11 'focus appearance' specify contrast?" | B:explanation: focus indicator ≥ 2 CSS px solid outline + 3:1 contrast vs unfocused | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1115 | C/0.22/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS focus-visible differ from focus pseudo-class?" | B:explanation: focus-visible = browser heuristic for keyboard focus; focus = any focus including click | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1116 | C/0.20/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS subgrid differ from regular grid?" | B:explanation: subgrid inherits parent grid tracks; aligns child rows/columns to parent | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1117 | C/0.18/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does view-transitions API enable cross-route animation?" | B:explanation: snapshot before/after; CSS pseudo-elements expose old/new for transition | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1118 | C/0.21/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS color-mix() compose colors?" | B:explanation: color-mix(in lch, A 60%, B 40%) interpolates in named color space | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1119 | C/0.19/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS @scope limit selector reach?" | B:explanation: @scope (root) to (limit) confines descendant matches; donut-scope semantics | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1120 | C/0.22/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React useId generate stable cross-server-client IDs?" | B:explanation: tree-aware counter that produces same id on server + client; safe for SSR | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1121 | C/0.20/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React useSyncExternalStore subscribe to external store?" | B:explanation: subscribe + getSnapshot; tear-aware in concurrent rendering | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1122 | C/0.18/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React Forms 'use' hook handle async data?" | B:explanation: 'use' suspends until promise resolves; integrates with Suspense for streaming | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1123 | C/0.21/3.6] svelte-docs | A:"How does Svelte 5 runes differ from $: reactive declarations?" | B:explanation: runes = explicit $state/$derived/$effect; $: was implicit and analysis-fragile | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1124 | C/0.19/3.6] vue-docs | A:"How does Vue defineModel macro replace v-model boilerplate?" | B:explanation: defineModel returns ref bound to v-model; eliminates emit/prop dance | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1125 | C/0.22/3.6] angular-docs | A:"How does Angular control-flow @if/@for differ from *ngIf/*ngFor?" | B:explanation: built-in control-flow = compiler-native, less indirection, smaller bundle | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1126 | C/0.20/3.6] tailwind-docs | A:"How does Tailwind container-queries plugin work?" | B:explanation: registers @container CSS rule emission; classes like @md:flex apply per parent size | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1127 | C/0.18/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS :has() pseudo-class enable parent selectors?" | B:explanation: matches element if it has descendants matching selector; e.g., li:has(>img) | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1128 | C/0.21/3.6] css-spec | A:"How does CSS @starting-style enable enter animations?" | B:explanation: declares starting values for first render; animates from there to current style | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1129 | C/0.19/3.6] react-docs | A:"How does React Compiler eliminate manual memoisation?" | B:explanation: compile-time analysis inserts memo where pure; reduces useMemo/useCallback boilerplate | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1130 | C/0.22/3.6] aria-spec | A:"How does ARIA aria-busy interact with screen readers?" | B:explanation: aria-busy=true suppresses live-region announcements until busy=false | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §14 — QA automation tool internals (rows 1131-1170)

[CODEQ-1131 | C/0.20/5.1] playwright-docs | A:"How does Playwright auto-wait choose strategies per locator?" | B:explanation: visibility + stability + actionability checks per element type before action | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1132 | C/0.18/5.1] playwright-docs | A:"How does Playwright trace viewer reconstruct test failures?" | B:explanation: snapshots + actions + console + network bundled in zip; viewer renders interactively | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1133 | C/0.21/5.1] playwright-docs | A:"How does Playwright projects enable cross-browser parallelism?" | B:explanation: project per browser; sharded across workers; per-project config inherits | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1134 | C/0.19/5.1] cypress-docs | A:"How does Cypress retryability differ from auto-wait?" | B:explanation: retryability = retry assertion until pass or timeout; auto-wait = command-level wait | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1135 | C/0.22/5.1] cypress-docs | A:"How does Cypress origin command lift same-origin restriction?" | B:explanation: cy.origin scopes commands to other origin; allows cross-domain flows in single test | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1136 | C/0.20/5.1] jest-docs | A:"How does Jest fake timers replace global timer functions?" | B:explanation: replaces setTimeout/setInterval/Date with controllable mocks; advance via runAllTimers | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1137 | C/0.18/5.1] vitest-docs | A:"How does Vitest differ from Jest in test isolation?" | B:explanation: Vitest = vite-native + ESM-first + worker isolation; Jest = require-based + custom runtime | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1138 | C/0.21/5.1] junit-docs | A:"How does JUnit 5 @TestInstance affect lifecycle?" | B:explanation: PER_METHOD = new instance per test; PER_CLASS = single instance reused | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1139 | C/0.19/5.1] junit-docs | A:"How does JUnit ParameterizedTest source providers work?" | B:explanation: @MethodSource/@CsvSource/@EnumSource each provide test arguments per invocation | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1140 | C/0.22/5.1] xunit-docs | A:"How does xUnit IClassFixture differ from ICollectionFixture?" | B:explanation: ClassFixture = shared per test class; CollectionFixture = shared across collection | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1141 | C/0.20/5.1] testcontainers-docs | A:"How does Testcontainers shared singleton work?" | B:explanation: container started once per JVM/Node process; reused across tests via static holder | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1142 | C/0.18/5.1] testcontainers-docs | A:"How does Testcontainers Ryuk handle container cleanup?" | B:explanation: Ryuk container reaps test containers on session end; prevents orphan containers | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1143 | C/0.21/5.1] mocha-docs | A:"How does Mocha root-hook ordering work across files?" | B:explanation: root hooks aggregate via root-hook-plugin; ordering by file load order | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1144 | C/0.19/5.1] selenium-docs | A:"How does Selenium 4 BiDi protocol differ from JSON-Wire?" | B:explanation: BiDi = bi-directional WebSocket events; JSON-Wire = sync REST | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1145 | C/0.22/5.1] cucumber-docs | A:"How does Cucumber data-table parameter binding work?" | B:explanation: column headers map to fields; rows iterated; types coerced per registered transformer | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1146 | C/0.20/5.1] k6-docs | A:"How does k6 stages model load ramp?" | B:explanation: stages = list of (target, duration); k6 ramps VUs linearly between targets | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1147 | C/0.18/5.1] k6-docs | A:"How does k6 thresholds gate test outcome?" | B:explanation: per-metric expression evaluated at end; failure marks test failed | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1148 | C/0.21/5.1] gatling-docs | A:"How does Gatling injection profile differ from k6 stages?" | B:explanation: Gatling = profile DSL (rampUsersPerSec, etc); k6 = imperative stages | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1149 | C/0.19/5.1] storybook-docs | A:"How does Storybook 8 test integration differ from Storybook 7?" | B:explanation: 8 ships test addon with portable interaction-tests; 7 needed external runner | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1150 | C/0.22/5.1] postman-docs | A:"How does Postman pre-request script differ from test script?" | B:explanation: pre = before send (set tokens etc); test = after response (assert + chain) | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1151 | C/0.20/5.1] pact-docs | A:"How does Pact provider verification work?" | B:explanation: provider replays consumer-recorded interactions against real impl; pass/fail per interaction | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1152 | C/0.18/5.1] pact-docs | A:"How does Pact Broker tag releases?" | B:explanation: tags pact versions with consumer/provider names + branch labels for can-i-deploy queries | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1153 | C/0.21/5.1] stryker-docs | A:"How does Stryker mutation testing measure code quality?" | B:explanation: applies mutators; runs tests; mutation killed = good test; mutation survived = gap | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1154 | C/0.19/5.1] approvaltests-docs | A:"How does Approval Tests handle test verification?" | B:explanation: test produces output → diffs vs received vs approved file → human approves change | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1155 | C/0.22/5.1] hypothesis-docs | A:"How does Hypothesis property-based testing shrink failures?" | B:explanation: shrink finds minimal counter-example by reducing input until still failing | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1156 | C/0.20/5.1] aXe-docs | A:"How does aXe-core scan for accessibility issues?" | B:explanation: rule-based DOM analysis with WCAG mapping; reports impact + element + remediation | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1157 | C/0.18/5.1] zap-docs | A:"How does ZAP active scan differ from passive scan?" | B:explanation: active = sends payloads to detect vulnerabilities; passive = analyses observed traffic | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1158 | C/0.21/5.1] sonarqube-docs | A:"How does SonarQube quality gate evaluate per pull request?" | B:explanation: per-PR new-code metrics gated; fails on critical issues or coverage drop | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1159 | C/0.19/5.1] github-actions-docs | A:"How does GitHub Actions matrix expansion handle include/exclude?" | B:explanation: cartesian product generated; include adds combos; exclude removes; max 256 jobs | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1160 | C/0.22/5.1] gitlab-ci-docs | A:"How does GitLab CI parallel-matrix run with resource_group?" | B:explanation: matrix expansion + resource_group serialises selected jobs across pipeline | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1161 | C/0.20/5.1] jenkins-docs | A:"How does Jenkins pipeline 'options' affect retention?" | B:explanation: buildDiscarder + timeout + skipDefaultCheckout per pipeline; overrides global defaults | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1162 | C/0.18/5.1] dind-docs | A:"How does Docker-in-Docker differ from Docker socket binding?" | B:explanation: DinD = nested daemon (heavy); socket bind = host daemon access (security risk) | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1163 | C/0.21/5.1] kubeval-docs | A:"How does kubeval differ from kubeconform?" | B:explanation: kubeval = legacy validator; kubeconform = faster + supports CRDs via JSON Schema | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1164 | C/0.19/5.1] terratest-docs | A:"How does Terratest verify Terraform deployments?" | B:explanation: Go test runs terraform apply; asserts via SDK; cleans up via destroy | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1165 | C/0.22/5.1] dependabot-docs | A:"How does Dependabot grouped updates differ from per-package?" | B:explanation: grouped = single PR for related deps; per-package = one PR per dep (default) | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1166 | C/0.20/5.1] renovate-docs | A:"How does Renovate Bot's preset config differ from explicit config?" | B:explanation: preset = curated extends like config:base; explicit = full inline rules | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1167 | C/0.18/5.1] codeql-docs | A:"How does CodeQL differ from grep-based static analysis?" | B:explanation: CodeQL = relational query over AST + dataflow; grep = textual pattern only | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1168 | C/0.21/5.1] semgrep-docs | A:"How does Semgrep differ from regex-based linting?" | B:explanation: Semgrep = AST-aware patterns + metavariables + dataflow; regex = string only | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1169 | C/0.19/5.1] grype-docs | A:"How does Grype scan SBOM for vulnerabilities?" | B:explanation: matches package versions in SBOM against vuln database; reports per-package | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1170 | C/0.22/5.1] trivy-docs | A:"How does Trivy differ from Grype for image scanning?" | B:explanation: Trivy = multi-target (image/IaC/secret); Grype = SBOM/image focused | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §15 — Project management methodology and architecture concepts (rows 1171-1210)

[CODEQ-1171 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the arc42 §1 'Goals and Constraints' differ from §3 'Context'?" | B:explanation: §1 = quality goals + drivers; §3 = system boundary + external systems | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1172 | C/0.18/2.1] adr-tools | A:"How does an ADR differ from an RFC document?" | B:explanation: ADR = single architectural decision per file; RFC = proposal-discuss-accept process | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1173 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does C4 model context level differ from container level?" | B:explanation: context = system + actors + external; container = internal deployable units inside system | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1174 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does C4 component level differ from code level?" | B:explanation: component = grouped responsibilities inside container; code = class-level details | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1175 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does an OKR differ from a KPI?" | B:explanation: OKR = aspirational outcome with key-result targets; KPI = ongoing operational metric | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1176 | C/0.20/2.1] scrum-guide | A:"How does Scrum sprint commitment differ from forecast?" | B:explanation: forecast = realistic estimate of what fits; commitment = locked promise (deprecated) | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1177 | C/0.18/2.1] kanban-method | A:"How does Kanban WIP limit affect throughput?" | B:explanation: WIP cap reduces context-switching; Little's law links WIP × cycle-time = throughput | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1178 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a risk register entry differ from an issue ticket?" | B:explanation: risk = future probability + impact; issue = current problem to resolve | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1179 | C/0.19/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does SLI differ from SLO and SLA?" | B:explanation: SLI = measure; SLO = internal target; SLA = customer commitment with consequences | P:PLAN/3.5 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1180 | C/0.22/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does error budget differ from SLO?" | B:explanation: SLO = target percentage; error budget = 100% − SLO available for unreliability | P:PLAN/3.5 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1181 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a runbook differ from a playbook?" | B:explanation: runbook = procedure for recurring task; playbook = response procedure for incident type | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1182 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a post-incident review (PIR) differ from a retrospective?" | B:explanation: PIR = incident-specific timeline + root cause + actions; retro = team-process review | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1183 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a stakeholder map differ from a RACI matrix?" | B:explanation: stakeholder map = influence/interest grid; RACI = role per task (responsible/accountable/etc) | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1184 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Pareto analysis differ from Ishikawa diagram?" | B:explanation: Pareto = 80/20 frequency ranking; Ishikawa = cause-and-effect tree | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1185 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a quality gate differ from a definition of done?" | B:explanation: quality gate = automated/specific check pass; DoD = team-agreed completion criteria | P:PLAN/5.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1186 | C/0.20/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does burn-rate alert differ from threshold alert?" | B:explanation: burn-rate = SLO budget consumed/time-window; threshold = static metric crossing | P:PLAN/3.5 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1187 | C/0.18/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does fast-burn vs slow-burn alert window differ?" | B:explanation: fast-burn = 1h budget exhaustion; slow-burn = 6h-3d sustained drain | P:PLAN/3.5 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1188 | C/0.21/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does CALMS framework summarise DevOps?" | B:explanation: Culture, Automation, Lean, Measurement, Sharing — DevOps adoption pillars | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1189 | C/0.19/2.1] dora-research | A:"How do DORA's 4 keys measure delivery performance?" | B:explanation: deployment frequency + lead time + change failure rate + MTTR | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1190 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Conway's law affect microservice boundaries?" | B:explanation: system structure mirrors org communication; team ownership ↔ service boundary | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1191 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Inverse Conway maneuver work?" | B:explanation: shape teams to desired architecture; team boundaries drive system boundaries | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1192 | C/0.18/2.1] tom-d-marco | A:"How does the Cone of Uncertainty narrow over time?" | B:explanation: estimate range tightens as project advances; early estimates have wide bands | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1193 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a pre-mortem differ from a post-mortem?" | B:explanation: pre-mortem = imagine future failure + identify causes pre-launch; post-mortem = analyse actual failure | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1194 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the change-advisory-board (CAB) differ from continuous deployment?" | B:explanation: CAB = manual approval gate per change; CD = automated gates with same rigor | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1195 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does feature toggle 'release' differ from 'experiment'?" | B:explanation: release toggle = decouple deploy from release; experiment toggle = A/B with measurement | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1196 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does blue-green deployment differ from canary?" | B:explanation: blue-green = atomic switch with full second env; canary = progressive traffic shift | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1197 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does shift-left testing differ from shift-right?" | B:explanation: shift-left = earlier in pipeline (unit/int); shift-right = production observability + chaos | P:PLAN/5.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1198 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does technical debt quadrant (Fowler) classify debt?" | B:explanation: deliberate-prudent / deliberate-reckless / inadvertent-prudent / inadvertent-reckless | P:PLAN/4.4 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1199 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does WSJF (Weighted Shortest Job First) prioritise work?" | B:explanation: cost-of-delay / job-size; SAFe-recommended for portfolio prioritisation | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1200 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does ICE scoring differ from RICE scoring?" | B:explanation: ICE = impact × confidence × ease; RICE adds reach (Reach × Impact × Confidence / Effort) | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1201 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does MoSCoW prioritisation order requirements?" | B:explanation: Must / Should / Could / Won't (this time); explicit acceptance scope | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1202 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the Kano model classify features?" | B:explanation: must-have / performance / delight / indifferent / reverse — drives prioritisation | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1203 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the value-proposition canvas relate to the BMC?" | B:explanation: VPC zooms into customer-segment + value-prop blocks of Business Model Canvas | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1204 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Discovery differ from Delivery in product development?" | B:explanation: Discovery = problem/solution validation; Delivery = build the validated solution | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1205 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a flow-efficiency metric differ from a utilisation metric?" | B:explanation: flow efficiency = active time / total cycle; utilisation = busy time / capacity | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1206 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does an SLA penalty clause differ from an error budget burn?" | B:explanation: SLA penalty = customer credit/refund; error budget = internal release decision input | P:PLAN/3.5 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1207 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does ITIL change-management classify changes?" | B:explanation: standard (pre-approved) / normal (CAB review) / emergency (urgent CAB review) | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1208 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does ITIL incident differ from problem?" | B:explanation: incident = single unplanned interruption; problem = underlying cause of incidents | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1209 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does sprint goal differ from sprint backlog?" | B:explanation: sprint goal = one-sentence outcome; sprint backlog = items selected to achieve goal | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1210 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does definition-of-ready differ from definition-of-done?" | B:explanation: DoR = entry criteria for sprint; DoD = exit criteria for completion | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §16 — Functional analysis + cross-domain (rows 1211-1250)

[CODEQ-1211 | C/0.20/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a domain entity differ from a DTO?" | B:explanation: entity = identity-bearing domain object with behaviour; DTO = transport shape no behaviour | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1212 | C/0.18/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a Value Object enforce equality?" | B:explanation: equality by structural value match (all fields); typically immutable | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1213 | C/0.21/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an aggregate enforce invariants?" | B:explanation: root entity validates state changes; persists transactionally; only-via-root access | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1214 | C/0.19/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a use-case differ from a user-story?" | B:explanation: use-case = formal goal + actors + flow; user-story = narrative who/what/why | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1215 | C/0.22/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an acceptance criterion differ from a Gate B?" | B:explanation: AC = user-visible behaviour assertion; Gate B = dispatchable measurement (count/threshold) | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1216 | C/0.20/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an event differ from a command in event-driven architecture?" | B:explanation: command = imperative request to do; event = past-tense fact emitted | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1217 | C/0.18/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an integration event differ from a domain event?" | B:explanation: domain event = inside bounded context; integration event = cross-context transport | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1218 | C/0.21/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a saga differ from a long-running workflow?" | B:explanation: saga = compensating distributed tx; workflow = orchestrated multi-step business process | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1219 | C/0.19/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a domain-event handler differ from an integration-event handler?" | B:explanation: domain-handler runs in-context; integration-handler reacts cross-context with own state | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1220 | C/0.22/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does CQRS read-model handle deletes?" | B:explanation: tombstone marker or soft-delete flag; projector applies on read-model | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1221 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-9457 | A:"How does problem-detail JSON shape allow extensions?" | B:explanation: required fields + free-form custom members; consumers ignore unknown fields | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1222 | C/0.18/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does HTTP Message Signature support post-quantum migration?" | B:explanation: signature-input lists params; alg parameter selectable; key-id versions per alg | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1223 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP nonce protect against captured proofs?" | B:explanation: server issues nonce; client must include in next proof; window-binds | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1224 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does JWT 'aud' claim handle multi-audience tokens?" | B:explanation: aud is string or string-array; verifier must match at least one expected aud | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1225 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does a security boundary differ from a trust zone?" | B:explanation: security boundary = enforced perimeter; trust zone = level of assumed integrity | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1226 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does a defense-in-depth model layer controls?" | B:explanation: multiple independent controls — failure of one doesn't compromise system | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1227 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does threat modelling using STRIDE classify threats?" | B:explanation: Spoof / Tamper / Repudiate / Info-disclose / DoS / Elevate-privilege | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1228 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does PASTA threat-modelling differ from STRIDE?" | B:explanation: PASTA = process-aligned 7 stages; STRIDE = checklist by attack class | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1229 | C/0.19/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does data classification feed retention policy?" | B:explanation: class drives retention duration + encryption + access controls | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1230 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does a privacy threshold assessment trigger DPIA?" | B:explanation: PTA = preliminary; if high-risk, DPIA = full data protection impact analysis | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1231 | C/0.20/3.5] otel | A:"How does observability differ from monitoring?" | B:explanation: monitoring = predefined dashboards/alerts; observability = explore unknowns via telemetry | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1232 | C/0.18/3.5] otel | A:"How do RED metrics differ from USE metrics?" | B:explanation: RED = Rate/Errors/Duration (services); USE = Utilisation/Saturation/Errors (resources) | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1233 | C/0.21/3.5] otel | A:"How does golden-signals model define the four signals?" | B:explanation: latency / traffic / errors / saturation — Google SRE four-signals model | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1234 | C/0.19/3.5] otel | A:"How does service-level indicator (SLI) differ from a metric?" | B:explanation: SLI = directly measures user-perceived behaviour; metric = any measurement (incl internal) | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1235 | C/0.22/3.5] otel | A:"How does a trace differ from a span?" | B:explanation: trace = end-to-end request; span = single unit of work in trace; trace contains spans | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1236 | C/0.20/3.5] otel | A:"How does W3C trace-context propagate across services?" | B:explanation: traceparent header + tracestate header; carries trace_id + span_id + flags | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1237 | C/0.18/3.5] otel | A:"How does baggage propagation differ from tracing context?" | B:explanation: baggage = arbitrary key/value across services; tracing context = id-only correlation | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1238 | C/0.21/3.5] otel | A:"How does OTel semconv define HTTP server route attribute?" | B:explanation: http.route = route template (e.g., /users/:id); avoids high-cardinality URL | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1239 | C/0.19/3.5] otel | A:"How does OpenTelemetry handle resource attribute hierarchy?" | B:explanation: explicit > env > detector > SDK default; merge per spec rules | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1240 | C/0.22/3.5] otel | A:"How does an exemplar in metric instrumentation work?" | B:explanation: optional trace-id attached to metric data point; links series to specific trace | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1241 | C/0.20/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does a multi-window multi-burn-rate alert work?" | B:explanation: 2-3 time windows (1h/6h/3d) with different burn rates; reduce false-positives | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1242 | C/0.18/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does adaptive sampling differ from fixed-rate?" | B:explanation: adaptive = adjusts rate dynamically based on load; fixed = constant rate | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1243 | C/0.21/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does tail-based sampling decide which traces to keep?" | B:explanation: collector waits for full trace; decision based on errors/latency criteria | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1244 | C/0.19/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does SLO error-budget policy gate releases?" | B:explanation: budget exhausted → freeze risky changes; budget healthy → permit experiments | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1245 | C/0.22/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does cardinality budget interact with metric design?" | B:explanation: high-card labels (user_id) explode storage; budget caps to maintain perf | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1246 | C/0.20/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does black-box monitoring differ from white-box?" | B:explanation: black-box = external probes; white-box = internal app metrics | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1247 | C/0.18/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does symptom-based alerting differ from cause-based?" | B:explanation: symptom = user-visible problem; cause = root condition; alert on symptoms | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1248 | C/0.21/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does on-call rotation reduce alert fatigue?" | B:explanation: load distribution + escalation paths + rotation cadence + post-incident review | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1249 | C/0.19/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does post-incident-review derive corrective action?" | B:explanation: timeline + 5-whys + contributing factors + actionable items + owners + due dates | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1250 | C/0.22/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does a chaos-engineering experiment differ from a load test?" | B:explanation: chaos = inject controlled failure to test resilience; load = saturate with normal load | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §17 — More design pattern explanations (rows 1251-1290)

[CODEQ-1251 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Strangler Fig pattern differ from Big-Bang rewrite?" | B:explanation: incremental traffic-shift behind facade; Big-Bang replaces atomically | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1252 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Process Manager differ from Saga orchestrator?" | B:explanation: Process Manager has explicit state machine + observable workflow; Saga decentralised | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1253 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Sharding differ from Partitioning?" | B:explanation: sharding = horizontal split across nodes; partitioning = within single node | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1254 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Cache-Aside differ from Read-Through cache?" | B:explanation: cache-aside = app fetches + populates; read-through = cache fetches on miss | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1255 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Pipes-and-Filters pattern compose ETL stages?" | B:explanation: each filter has stage contract; pipes pass data + buffer; back-pressure flows upstream | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1256 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Health Endpoint Monitoring split /health, /readiness, /liveness?" | B:explanation: liveness = process up; readiness = dependencies up; health = aggregate snapshot | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1257 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Leader Election achieve coordination?" | B:explanation: nodes elect leader via consensus (Raft/Paxos); leader-only-tasks; failover on leader loss | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1258 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Pub/Sub topic differ from queue?" | B:explanation: topic = multi-subscriber broadcast; queue = single-consumer (work distribution) | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1259 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Materialised View pattern handle staleness?" | B:explanation: refresh cadence + staleness budget + reader cope strategy | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1260 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Polling Consumer differ from Push Subscriber?" | B:explanation: polling = consumer pulls on cadence; push = broker delivers when message available | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1261 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Gateway Aggregation reduce chatty clients?" | B:explanation: gateway combines multiple backend calls into single response; reduces network round-trips | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1262 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Federated Identity decouple identity from app?" | B:explanation: app trusts external IdP via tokens; users centralise credentials at IdP | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1263 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Valet Key pattern issue temporary access?" | B:explanation: server signs URL with scope + expiry; client uses URL directly without auth round-trip | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1264 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Claim-Check pattern handle large messages?" | B:explanation: store payload in object-store; pass reference through queue; consumer fetches via reference | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1265 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Priority Queue prevent starvation?" | B:explanation: aging boost (priority increases with wait time) or fairness budget per priority class | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1266 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Sequencer pattern enforce global ordering?" | B:explanation: dedicated node assigns global sequence numbers; consumers process in order | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1267 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Aggregator pattern reconcile multi-source data?" | B:explanation: collect from sources; resolve conflicts by rule; emit canonical result | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1268 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Routing Slip pattern enable dynamic flows?" | B:explanation: each step has next-step routing rule; slip travels with message; flow dynamic per message | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1269 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Scatter-Gather aggregate parallel queries?" | B:explanation: fan-out concurrent requests; collect; merge or fail-fast on partial results | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1270 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Resequencer fix out-of-order events?" | B:explanation: buffer with ordering window; deliver in order; drop or DLQ stale events | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1271 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Splitter + Aggregator handle batch operations?" | B:explanation: split batch into items; process in parallel; aggregator combines results | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1272 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Event Replay pattern rebuild read-model?" | B:explanation: checkpoint + sequential replay from event-store; idempotent application; resume on failure | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1273 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Materialised Path differ from Closure Table for hierarchy?" | B:explanation: materialised path = string column with all ancestors; closure table = separate join table | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1274 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Two-Phase Locking ensure serialisability?" | B:explanation: growing phase (acquire locks) + shrinking phase (release); no acquire after release | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1275 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Optimistic Concurrency Control detect conflicts?" | B:explanation: version-token compared on update; mismatch indicates concurrent change; retry on conflict | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1276 | C/0.20/3.1] cell-based-architecture | A:"How does cell-based architecture limit blast radius?" | B:explanation: traffic partitioned into cells; each cell isolated; failure stays within cell | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1277 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Anti-Corruption Layer prevent legacy concept leakage?" | B:explanation: translator maps legacy terms to new ubiquitous language at boundary; legacy stays inside | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1278 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Domain Event boundary differ from Integration Event?" | B:explanation: domain event = inside bounded context; integration event = cross-context transport | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1279 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Aggregate snapshot strategy handle ES-heavy systems?" | B:explanation: store snapshot every N events; reconstruct from snapshot + delta on read | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1280 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does API Composition handle cross-aggregate queries?" | B:explanation: composer fetches per aggregate; combines result; fall back to degraded shape on partial fail | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1281 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Circuit Breaker pattern transition states?" | B:explanation: closed → open on threshold; open → half-open after timeout; half-open → closed on success | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1282 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Throttling differ from Rate Limiting?" | B:explanation: throttling = slow down (queue/delay); rate limit = reject excess; both control flow | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1283 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Bulkhead pattern partition resources?" | B:explanation: thread/connection pools per upstream; failure in one bulkhead doesn't drain others | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1284 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Sidecar pattern decouple cross-cutting concerns?" | B:explanation: separate process per pod; language-agnostic; lifecycle tied to main app | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1285 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Idempotency Key handle replay safely?" | B:explanation: server stores key + result; same key returns stored result without re-processing | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1286 | C/0.20/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does CQRS handle eventual consistency?" | B:explanation: write succeeds → events emitted → projector updates read-model; UI polls/subscribes | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1287 | C/0.18/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Event Sourcing handle event versioning?" | B:explanation: upcaster transforms old events to new shape on read; explicit version tag per event | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1288 | C/0.21/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Hexagonal Architecture enforce dependency direction?" | B:explanation: domain has zero outbound deps; ports define contracts; adapters depend on domain | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1289 | C/0.19/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Saga compensation order differ from forward order?" | B:explanation: compensations run in reverse — last successful step first | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1290 | C/0.22/3.1] gof-patterns | A:"How does Outbox pattern guarantee event publication?" | B:explanation: write to outbox in same tx as state; relay reads + publishes; idempotent at consumer | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1335 | C/0.22/5.1] cypress-docs | A:"How does Cypress component testing differ from e2e mode?" | B:explanation: component = mount single component in isolation; e2e = real browser navigation | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1336 | C/0.20/5.1] cypress-docs | A:"How does Cypress dashboard parallelisation work?" | B:explanation: orchestrator distributes specs across machines; balances by past duration | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1337 | C/0.18/5.1] jest-docs | A:"How does Jest --watch mode detect related tests?" | B:explanation: Jest tracks file deps; runs tests touched by changed files | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1340 | C/0.22/5.1] vitest-docs | A:"How does Vitest browser mode differ from jsdom?" | B:explanation: browser mode runs in real browser via Playwright/WebdriverIO; jsdom = node-emulated | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1343 | C/0.21/5.1] testcontainers-docs | A:"How does Testcontainers GenericContainer differ from typed module?" | B:explanation: Generic = any image with manual config; typed module = curated config + waitStrategy | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1345 | C/0.22/5.1] selenium-docs | A:"How does Selenium Manager auto-resolve drivers?" | B:explanation: bundled binary detects browser; downloads matching driver; manages cache | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1346 | C/0.20/5.1] selenium-docs | A:"How does Selenium Grid 4 hub-router differ from old hub-node?" | B:explanation: 4 = router + distributor + session-map + node; 3 = single hub + nodes | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1347 | C/0.18/5.1] cucumber-docs | A:"How does Cucumber Background section reduce duplication?" | B:explanation: steps before each scenario; runs implicitly; replaces shared Given setup | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1348 | C/0.21/5.1] cucumber-docs | A:"How does Cucumber tag expressions filter scenarios?" | B:explanation: boolean expression over tags (and/or/not); selects matching scenarios | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1349 | C/0.19/5.1] k6-docs | A:"How does k6 'browser' module enable browser-based tests?" | B:explanation: integrated headless Chrome; combines protocol-level perf with browser interactions | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1350 | C/0.22/5.1] k6-docs | A:"How does k6 SLO checks integrate with Grafana k6 Cloud?" | B:explanation: thresholds defined in script; cloud reports per SLO; trends across runs | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1351 | C/0.20/5.1] gatling-docs | A:"How does Gatling Enterprise differ from open-source?" | B:explanation: Enterprise = central orchestration + reporting + auth; OSS = single-node + local report | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1352 | C/0.18/5.1] storybook-docs | A:"How does Storybook 8 testing addon replace storyshots?" | B:explanation: portable interaction tests run via vitest; storyshots was snapshot-only | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1353 | C/0.21/5.1] postman-docs | A:"How does Postman Newman CLI differ from collection runner UI?" | B:explanation: Newman = headless CLI for CI; UI = interactive desktop app for development | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1357 | C/0.18/5.1] hypothesis-docs | A:"How does Hypothesis stateful testing find race conditions?" | B:explanation: state-machine generates action sequences; explores combinations; shrinks counter-examples | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1361 | C/0.20/5.1] gitlab-ci-docs | A:"How does GitLab CI rules:exists trigger jobs by file presence?" | B:explanation: rule evaluates file glob existence; job runs only if matched | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §20 — More PM methodology + SRE + cross-domain (rows 1371-1410)

[CODEQ-1371 | C/0.20/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does SLO error-budget burn-rate alerting work?" | B:explanation: alert if budget burned faster than expected over short and long windows | P:PLAN/3.5 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1373 | C/0.21/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does an SRE blameless post-mortem differ from a blameful one?" | B:explanation: blameless focuses on systems + processes, never people; encourages psychological safety | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1374 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the wardley map differ from a value chain?" | B:explanation: Wardley plots components against evolution axis; value chain only sequences | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1375 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does inverse-thinking pre-mortem reveal hidden risks?" | B:explanation: imagine project failed; list reasons; addresses risks before they occur | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1376 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does fitness function-as-code anchor architecture invariants?" | B:explanation: executable assertions per architecture rule; CI fails on regression | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1377 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does architecture decision record (ADR) link to fitness functions?" | B:explanation: ADR cites tests/checks proving decision; CI enforces; drift surfaces in tests | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1378 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the Theory of Constraints inform delivery?" | B:explanation: identifies bottleneck; team focuses there; throughput limited by slowest step | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1379 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does cycle-time differ from lead-time in delivery?" | B:explanation: lead = idea to delivery; cycle = active work to delivery; lead ≥ cycle | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1380 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does WIP limit affect throughput per Little's law?" | B:explanation: throughput = WIP / cycle-time; reducing WIP at constant cycle-time improves flow | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1381 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Cynefin framework classify problem domains?" | B:explanation: clear / complicated / complex / chaotic / confusion; method per domain | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1382 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Agile Manifesto's 'working software' shape sprint review?" | B:explanation: demos prioritise running software over documentation; feedback loop tightened | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1383 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does product-trio differ from squad?" | B:explanation: trio = product manager + designer + tech lead; squad = full delivery team | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1384 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does North Star metric differ from OKR?" | B:explanation: NSM = single guiding outcome; OKRs = quarterly objectives + key results | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1385 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does jobs-to-be-done framing shape product discovery?" | B:explanation: focus on user goals + outcomes; not personas + features | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1386 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does an enabling team differ from platform team?" | B:explanation: enabling = mentor + transition; platform = ongoing service ownership | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1387 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a complicated-subsystem team differ from stream-aligned?" | B:explanation: complicated = specialised expertise; stream-aligned = end-to-end product slice | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1388 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Domain-Driven Design's 'context map' inform team boundary?" | B:explanation: bounded contexts = ownership boundaries; teams shouldn't span contexts | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1389 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the team-API concept work in Team Topologies?" | B:explanation: explicit interface (Slack channel, docs, on-call) reduces cross-team friction | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1390 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the X-team-model encourage flow?" | B:explanation: stream-aligned does product work; platform/enabling/complicated support the streams | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1391 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does HTTP message signing prevent payload tampering in transit?" | B:explanation: HMAC over canonicalised request components; verifier rejects on mismatch | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1392 | C/0.18/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP token-binding mitigate stolen-token replay?" | B:explanation: token bound to client public key; proof signed per request; replay fails without key | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1393 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-9457 | A:"How does problem-detail's instance field differ from type?" | B:explanation: type = problem class URI (stable); instance = specific occurrence URI (per-request) | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1394 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does JWT encrypted (JWE) differ from signed (JWS)?" | B:explanation: JWS = signed but readable; JWE = encrypted (confidentiality + integrity) | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1395 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-8725 | A:"How does JWT BCP recommend handling unknown crit headers?" | B:explanation: reject token; BCP §2.5 mandates strict crit handling | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1396 | C/0.20/3.4] csp-l3 | A:"How does CSP nonce protect against XSS?" | B:explanation: random nonce required on each script tag; injected scripts lack nonce; blocked | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1397 | C/0.18/3.4] cwe-79 | A:"How does Trusted Types API mitigate DOM XSS?" | B:explanation: forces sink calls to use vetted types; raw strings rejected at DOM boundary | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1398 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-9116 | A:"How does security.txt's PGP signing increase trust?" | B:explanation: signature proves authorship; consumers verify against published key | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1399 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-7591 | A:"How does dynamic-client-registration's software_statement work?" | B:explanation: signed JWT with metadata; server trusts based on issuer; reduces manual config | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1400 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does mutual-TLS differ from one-way TLS?" | B:explanation: one-way = server cert verified by client; mutual = both verify each other | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1401 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does field-level encryption protect PII at rest?" | B:explanation: per-field encryption with separate key; database sees ciphertext only | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1402 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does envelope encryption pattern reduce KMS calls?" | B:explanation: data key encrypts payload; KMS encrypts data key; one KMS call per payload | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1403 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does crypto-shredding enable GDPR right-to-erasure?" | B:explanation: delete encryption key; ciphertext becomes unrecoverable; equivalent to deletion | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1404 | C/0.19/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does device-binding for high-risk operations work?" | B:explanation: enrol device fingerprint; tie token to device; require step-up on new device | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1405 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does OAuth-app revocation cascade work?" | B:explanation: revoke triggers refresh-token revoke + access-token blacklist + downstream notify | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1406 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does WebAuthn passwordless flow work?" | B:explanation: registers public key on device; authenticator signs server challenge; server verifies | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1407 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does passkey differ from WebAuthn credential?" | B:explanation: passkey = WebAuthn credential synced across devices via platform (iCloud/Google) | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1408 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does FIDO2 server attestation verify authenticator?" | B:explanation: authenticator signs attestation; server checks against trusted-authenticator metadata | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1409 | C/0.19/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does HSTS preload protect against downgrade attack?" | B:explanation: browser hardcodes HTTPS-only domains; never falls back to plaintext | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1410 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does SameSite cookie attribute mitigate CSRF?" | B:explanation: SameSite=Lax/Strict prevents cookie on cross-site requests; eliminates CSRF vector | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §21 — More functional analysis + cross-domain (rows 1411-1450)

[CODEQ-1411 | C/0.20/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a domain entity's invariant differ from validation?" | B:explanation: invariant = always true (enforced by entity); validation = input check at boundary | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1412 | C/0.18/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a Use Case differ from a User Journey?" | B:explanation: use-case = goal + actor + flow; journey = end-to-end customer experience across systems | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1413 | C/0.21/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an Acceptance Criterion differ from a non-functional requirement?" | B:explanation: AC = functional behaviour assertion; NFR = quality attribute (performance, reliability) | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1414 | C/0.19/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a feature flag's release scope differ from experiment scope?" | B:explanation: release = decouple deploy from release; experiment = A/B with measurement | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1415 | C/0.22/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a saga step's compensation differ from rollback?" | B:explanation: compensation = forward-action that reverses effect; rollback = undoing tx state | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1416 | C/0.20/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does eventual-consistency budget shape UI design?" | B:explanation: budget = allowed lag; UI shows pending state until lag closed | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1417 | C/0.18/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does a domain event's publication time interact with persistence?" | B:explanation: event published after commit; in-tx publication risks dual-write inconsistency | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1418 | C/0.21/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an Aggregate's persistence boundary affect concurrency?" | B:explanation: one root = one transaction unit; multi-aggregate writes need saga or split tx | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1419 | C/0.19/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does Value Object equality enable replacement semantics?" | B:explanation: VOs equal by all fields; replace whole instance instead of mutating | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1420 | C/0.22/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does primitive-obsession leak domain rules?" | B:explanation: raw types (string id, decimal money) scatter rules across services; type-safe wrappers consolidate | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1421 | C/0.20/3.5] otel | A:"How does service-mesh telemetry differ from app-instrumented telemetry?" | B:explanation: mesh = transport-level (L4/L7) + uniform; app = business semantics + custom | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
