[CODEQ-1435 | C/0.22/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does an empty-state with onboarding helpers improve activation?" | B:explanation: illustration + CTA + first-step guide; reduces blank-screen confusion | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1436 | C/0.20/5.1] arc42 | A:"How does flake-budget policy keep test suite stable?" | B:explanation: cap on flaky-rate (e.g., 2%); exceedance triggers quarantine + investigation SLA | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1437 | C/0.18/5.1] arc42 | A:"How does test-pyramid ratio affect feedback time?" | B:explanation: many fast unit + few slow e2e = quick feedback; inverted = slow + brittle | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1438 | C/0.21/5.1] arc42 | A:"How does a contract-test broker enable independent deploys?" | B:explanation: stores pacts; can-i-deploy queries safety; teams release without coordination | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1439 | C/0.19/5.1] arc42 | A:"How does chaos-engineering minimum-viable hypothesis work?" | B:explanation: state expected steady; inject fault; verify steady survives or fix surfaces | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1440 | C/0.22/5.1] arc42 | A:"How does property-based testing find edge cases unit tests miss?" | B:explanation: generators sample input space; shrink finds minimal counter-example | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1441 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does an API gateway differ from an edge function?" | B:explanation: gateway = managed routing + auth + rate-limit; edge function = compute at PoP | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1442 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does a service mesh differ from API gateway?" | B:explanation: mesh = service-to-service east-west traffic; gateway = north-south ingress | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1443 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does a data lake differ from a data warehouse?" | B:explanation: lake = raw data + schema-on-read; warehouse = curated + schema-on-write | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1444 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does a lakehouse architecture combine lake and warehouse?" | B:explanation: ACID over object-store (Delta/Iceberg); schema enforcement + cheap storage | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1445 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does a data mesh differ from data lake?" | B:explanation: mesh = domain-owned data products + federated governance; lake = central blob | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1446 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does CDC (Change Data Capture) differ from ETL?" | B:explanation: CDC = streaming row-level changes; ETL = batch full/incremental table loads | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1447 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does Stream Processing differ from Batch Processing?" | B:explanation: stream = continuous + low-latency; batch = bounded data + high-throughput | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1448 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does a feature store differ from a data lake?" | B:explanation: feature store = curated ML features + online/offline serving; lake = raw data | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1449 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does a vector database differ from a relational database?" | B:explanation: vector DB = similarity search via ANN; relational DB = exact match + joins | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1450 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does a content-addressed storage differ from path-addressed?" | B:explanation: CAS = key derived from content hash; immutable; deduplicated | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §22 — More design pattern + AI/ML internals (rows 1451-1490)

[CODEQ-1451 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does data-mesh principle 'data as product' differ from data-as-asset?" | B:explanation: data-as-product = treated like external product (SLA, doc, owner); asset = internal artefact | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1452 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does lakehouse differ from data lake structurally?" | B:explanation: lakehouse adds ACID + schema enforcement (Delta/Iceberg) over object-store; lake is raw blob | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1453 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does CDC handle schema-evolution downstream?" | B:explanation: schema-registry tracks versions; consumer reads with compatibility upcaster | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1454 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does feature-store online vs offline serving differ?" | B:explanation: online = sub-ms low-latency for inference; offline = batch retrieval for training | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1455 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does vector-database approximate nearest-neighbour (ANN) work?" | B:explanation: index (HNSW/IVF) trades exact accuracy for low latency; configurable recall/perf | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1456 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does content-addressed storage enable dedup?" | B:explanation: hash of content as key; identical content writes to same key; reference counted | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1457 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does time-series database optimise write path?" | B:explanation: append-only + columnar compression + per-time-shard files; rare updates | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1458 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does graph database differ from relational for many-to-many?" | B:explanation: graph DB stores edges natively; relational requires join tables; graph faster on traversal | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1459 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does RAG (Retrieval-Augmented Generation) ground LLM output?" | B:explanation: retrieve relevant docs first; pass to model as context; reduces hallucination | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1460 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does fine-tuning differ from RAG for domain-specific LLM?" | B:explanation: fine-tuning bakes knowledge into weights; RAG retrieves at inference; trade-offs differ | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1461 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does prompt-engineering differ from prompt-template versioning?" | B:explanation: prompt-engineering = ad-hoc design; versioning = registry + eval + rollback | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1462 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does feature-flag for ML model differ from feature-flag for code?" | B:explanation: ML flag selects model version; code flag selects code branch; same gating mechanism | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1463 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does drift detection differ from anomaly detection?" | B:explanation: drift = distribution shift over time; anomaly = single outlier event | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1464 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does federated learning preserve data privacy?" | B:explanation: training happens on-device; only gradients/updates aggregated centrally | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1465 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does differential privacy add noise without breaking utility?" | B:explanation: calibrated noise based on epsilon; protects individuals while preserving aggregate stats | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1466 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does synthetic data differ from anonymised real data?" | B:explanation: synthetic generated from distribution; anonymised redacts real records; privacy trade-offs differ | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1467 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI watermarking enable provenance tracking?" | B:explanation: embed signature in output (text/image); verify at consumption | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1468 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI explainability differ from interpretability?" | B:explanation: interpretability = inherent (decision tree); explainability = post-hoc (SHAP/LIME) | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1469 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does eventual consistency budget compare to strong consistency cost?" | B:explanation: eventual budget = perceived staleness; strong = global lock cost; CAP trade-off | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1470 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does Lambda architecture's batch + speed layer reconcile?" | B:explanation: serving layer merges latest from speed with corrected from batch; eventually consistent | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1471 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does Kappa architecture replay events without batch layer?" | B:explanation: full-fidelity stream; reprocess by re-feeding from start; needs idempotency | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1472 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does SCD-Type-2 differ from SCD-Type-1?" | B:explanation: Type-2 = preserve history with valid-from/to; Type-1 = overwrite | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1473 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does data-lineage benefit compliance?" | B:explanation: prove data origin + transformations; supports audits + DSAR + retention | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1474 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does master-data-management resolve duplicates across sources?" | B:explanation: golden record + reconciliation rules + steward override | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1475 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does customer-data-platform unify profiles across sources?" | B:explanation: identity resolution combines signals (email, device, behaviour) into unified profile | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1476 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does ML model-serving canary differ from code canary?" | B:explanation: traffic split by model version; metrics include accuracy/quality not just latency | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1477 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does shadow deployment differ from canary?" | B:explanation: shadow = mirrored traffic, results discarded; canary = subset of real traffic served | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1478 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI-cost budgeting per feature work?" | B:explanation: token counter + per-feature budget + alerting; rejects request when budget exceeded | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1479 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI-content moderation pipeline classify?" | B:explanation: stages: classifier → confidence threshold → manual review → appeal | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1480 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI-safety-evaluation gate work?" | B:explanation: evaluation suite + threshold + escalation; blocks release on regressions | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1481 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI-explainability for high-stakes decisions work?" | B:explanation: capture decision + factors + audit trail; appeal path required | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1482 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI-feedback loop improve model over time?" | B:explanation: capture user feedback + privacy filter + retraining queue + acceptance gate | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1483 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does prompt-injection differ from SQL-injection?" | B:explanation: prompt-injection bends LLM behaviour via crafted input; mitigations differ from SQL | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1484 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does LLM jailbreak differ from prompt-injection?" | B:explanation: jailbreak removes safety guardrails; prompt-injection redirects task | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1485 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does LLM hallucination differ from factual error?" | B:explanation: hallucination = confident plausible-but-wrong; factual error = stated incorrectly | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1486 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI red-teaming differ from black-box testing?" | B:explanation: red-team probes safety boundaries adversarially; black-box tests behaviour without internals | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1487 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does retrieval re-ranking improve RAG?" | B:explanation: re-ranker re-orders top-k retrievals by relevance; improves context quality | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1539 | C/0.19/5.1] xunit-docs | A:"How does xUnit Fact differ from Theory?" | B:explanation: Fact = one execution; Theory = parameterised, runs per data row | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1540 | C/0.22/5.1] testcontainers-docs | A:"How does Testcontainers withReuse mode persist containers?" | B:explanation: tags container with hash; reuses if same hash on next run | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1541 | C/0.20/5.1] testcontainers-docs | A:"How does Testcontainers Ryuk handle cleanup?" | B:explanation: privileged container reaps test containers on session end | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1542 | C/0.18/5.1] cucumber-docs | A:"How does Cucumber Hooks order execute?" | B:explanation: Before/After hooks run wrap step execution; tagged hooks filter by tag | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1543 | C/0.21/5.1] k6-docs | A:"How does k6 setup() function differ from default function?" | B:explanation: setup runs once before VUs start; passes data to default function | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1544 | C/0.19/5.1] gatling-docs | A:"How does Gatling closed-vs-open injection model differ?" | B:explanation: closed = fixed concurrent users; open = arrival rate of new users (more realistic) | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1545 | C/0.22/5.1] storybook-docs | A:"How does Storybook composition work for monorepos?" | B:explanation: refs config combines stories from multiple packages into single Storybook | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1546 | C/0.20/5.1] postman-docs | A:"How does Postman collection runner data file iteration work?" | B:explanation: each row spawns iteration with row vars; integrated with assertion engine | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1547 | C/0.18/5.1] pact-docs | A:"How does Pact Plug-in framework support new protocols?" | B:explanation: plugin registers protocol-specific matchers; broker stores plugin metadata | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1548 | C/0.21/5.1] stryker-docs | A:"How does Stryker incremental mode reduce test runs?" | B:explanation: caches mutation results; only re-runs affected mutants on changes | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1549 | C/0.19/5.1] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Step Functions Map state distributed mode work?" | B:explanation: distributed = up to 10K concurrent items; runs as child executions | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1550 | C/0.22/5.1] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Lambda SnapStart reduce cold starts for Java?" | B:explanation: snapshots initialised JVM; restores from snapshot on invocation | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1551 | C/0.20/5.1] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Lambda response streaming differ from buffered?" | B:explanation: streaming = chunks sent as available; buffered = wait for full response | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1552 | C/0.18/5.1] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Cloud Run min-instances differ from max-instances?" | B:explanation: min = always-warm; max = scale-up cap; ranges control cost vs latency | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1553 | C/0.21/5.1] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Pub/Sub schema-validation enforce types?" | B:explanation: schema attached at topic; producers must publish valid messages | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1554 | C/0.19/5.1] azure-docs | A:"How does Azure Service Bus Sessions enable ordered processing?" | B:explanation: messages with same SessionId locked to single receiver; FIFO within session | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1555 | C/0.22/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s Sidecar containers feature differ from init containers?" | B:explanation: sidecar = lifecycle tied to pod (always run); init = run once before main | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1556 | C/0.20/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s ValidatingAdmissionPolicy via CEL differ from ValidatingWebhook?" | B:explanation: VAP = native CEL evaluation; webhook = external HTTP service | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1557 | C/0.18/5.1] istio-docs | A:"How does Istio Ambient Mesh differ from sidecar mode?" | B:explanation: ambient = ztunnel + waypoint; sidecar = per-pod proxy; ambient lower overhead | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1558 | C/0.21/5.1] envoy-docs | A:"How does Envoy WASM filter extension work?" | B:explanation: WASM module loaded by Envoy; runs sandboxed filter logic per request | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1559 | C/0.19/5.1] linkerd-docs | A:"How does Linkerd Ambient Mesh differ from sidecar?" | B:explanation: planned future support; current Linkerd uses lightweight per-pod proxy | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1560 | C/0.22/5.1] cncf-docs | A:"How does CNCF Sandbox differ from Incubation differ from Graduation?" | B:explanation: sandbox = experimental; incubation = active community; graduation = mature governance | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1561 | C/0.20/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s Gateway API differ from Ingress?" | B:explanation: Gateway = role-based + extensible (more granular); Ingress = simple HTTP routing | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1562 | C/0.18/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s Pod Security Admission replace PodSecurityPolicy?" | B:explanation: PSA = built-in admission controller with profiles (privileged/baseline/restricted) | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1563 | C/0.21/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s ImageVolume feature mount OCI image as volume?" | B:explanation: alpha API; mounts OCI artifact (model, config) as read-only volume | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1564 | C/0.19/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s SchedulingGates pause scheduling?" | B:explanation: pod with gate stays unscheduled until gate cleared; useful for batch coordination | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1565 | C/0.22/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s ResourceClaim API differ from PVC?" | B:explanation: ResourceClaim = generic resource (GPU/network); PVC = storage-only | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1566 | C/0.20/5.1] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS VPC Lattice differ from Service Mesh?" | B:explanation: VPC Lattice = AWS-native cross-VPC application networking; mesh = Kubernetes-typically | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1567 | C/0.18/5.1] aws-docs | A:"How does AWS Verified Permissions integrate with Cedar?" | B:explanation: managed Cedar policy engine; integrates with Cognito/IAM identity | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1568 | C/0.21/5.1] gcp-docs | A:"How does GCP Workload Identity Federation replace service-account keys?" | B:explanation: external IdP token exchanged for short-lived GCP token; no static keys | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1570 | C/0.22/5.1] kubernetes-docs | A:"How does k8s admission webhook side-effects=NoneOnDryRun differ from None?" | B:explanation: NoneOnDryRun = side-effects only on real apply; None = pure validation no effects | P:ARCH/5.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §25 — More PM/SRE methodology + functional analysis (rows 1571-1610)

[CODEQ-1571 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the Pace-Layered application strategy classify systems?" | B:explanation: systems-of-record vs differentiation vs innovation; lifecycle differs per layer | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1572 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Wardley map's evolution axis (genesis-custom-product-commodity) inform strategy?" | B:explanation: components shift right; commodity = buy; genesis = build to differentiate | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1573 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does PACE-T cost model compare alternatives?" | B:explanation: people, asset, cost, ease, time — multi-axis trade-off framework | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1574 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the SAFe (Scaled Agile Framework) PI Planning differ from sprint planning?" | B:explanation: PI = quarterly cross-team; sprint = bi-weekly per team | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1575 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does LeSS (Large-Scale Scrum) differ from SAFe?" | B:explanation: LeSS = lighter framework, fewer roles; SAFe = more prescriptive with extra layers | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1576 | C/0.20/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does SRE error-budget policy gate releases?" | B:explanation: budget exhausted → freeze risky changes; budget healthy → permit experiments | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1577 | C/0.18/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does Toil-vs-Engineering ratio inform SRE staffing?" | B:explanation: target ≤50% toil; remaining must be engineering work; track and rebalance | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1578 | C/0.21/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does blameless post-mortem culture work?" | B:explanation: focus on systems + processes, never people; encourages psychological safety | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1579 | C/0.19/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does cap-and-trade error budget across teams work?" | B:explanation: teams with extra budget can trade with teams over budget; maintains org-level guarantees | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1580 | C/0.22/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does fast-burn vs slow-burn alert window differ?" | B:explanation: fast-burn = 1h budget exhaustion; slow-burn = 6h-3d sustained drain | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1581 | C/0.20/2.1] sre-book | A:"How does CALMS framework summarise DevOps?" | B:explanation: Culture, Automation, Lean, Measurement, Sharing — DevOps adoption pillars | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1582 | C/0.18/2.1] dora-research | A:"How do DORA's 4 keys measure delivery performance?" | B:explanation: deployment frequency + lead time + change failure rate + MTTR | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1583 | C/0.21/2.1] dora-research | A:"How does SPACE framework differ from DORA?" | B:explanation: SPACE = 5 dims (satisfaction/perf/activity/comm/efficiency); DORA = 4 outcome metrics | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1584 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Conway's law affect microservice boundaries?" | B:explanation: system structure mirrors org communication; team ownership ↔ service boundary | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1585 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Inverse Conway maneuver work?" | B:explanation: shape teams to desired architecture; team boundaries drive system boundaries | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1586 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the Cone of Uncertainty narrow over time?" | B:explanation: estimate range tightens as project advances; early estimates have wide bands | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1587 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does pre-mortem differ from post-mortem in practice?" | B:explanation: pre-mortem = imagine future failure + identify causes pre-launch; post = analyse actual | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1588 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a CAB (change-advisory-board) differ from continuous deployment?" | B:explanation: CAB = manual approval gate per change; CD = automated gates with same rigor | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1589 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does feature toggle 'release' differ from 'experiment'?" | B:explanation: release toggle = decouple deploy from release; experiment toggle = A/B with measurement | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1590 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does blue-green deployment differ from canary?" | B:explanation: blue-green = atomic switch with full second env; canary = progressive traffic shift | P:PLAN/3.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1591 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does shift-left testing differ from shift-right?" | B:explanation: shift-left = earlier in pipeline (unit/int); shift-right = production observability + chaos | P:PLAN/5.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1592 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does technical-debt quadrant (Fowler) classify debt?" | B:explanation: deliberate-prudent / deliberate-reckless / inadvertent-prudent / inadvertent-reckless | P:PLAN/4.4 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1593 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does WSJF (Weighted Shortest Job First) prioritise work?" | B:explanation: cost-of-delay / job-size; SAFe-recommended for portfolio prioritisation | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1594 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does ICE scoring differ from RICE scoring?" | B:explanation: ICE = impact × confidence × ease; RICE adds reach (Reach × Impact × Confidence / Effort) | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1595 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does the Kano model classify features?" | B:explanation: must-have / performance / delight / indifferent / reverse — drives prioritisation | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1596 | C/0.20/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an Aggregate enforce invariants?" | B:explanation: root entity validates state changes; persists transactionally; only-via-root access | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1597 | C/0.18/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does Value Object enforce equality?" | B:explanation: equality by structural value match (all fields); typically immutable | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1598 | C/0.21/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does Domain Service differ from Application Service?" | B:explanation: Domain Service = pure domain behaviour across aggregates; App Service = use-case orchestration | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1599 | C/0.19/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does Repository pattern differ from DAO?" | B:explanation: Repository = collection-like aggregate access; DAO = per-entity CRUD abstraction | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1600 | C/0.22/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does Specification pattern compose query rules?" | B:explanation: AND/OR/NOT operators combine specs; each spec implements isSatisfiedBy + toQuery | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1601 | C/0.20/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an Acceptance Criterion differ from non-functional requirement?" | B:explanation: AC = functional behaviour assertion; NFR = quality attribute (performance, reliability) | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1602 | C/0.18/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does an integration event differ from a domain event?" | B:explanation: domain event = inside bounded context; integration event = cross-context transport | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1603 | C/0.21/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does eventual-consistency budget shape UI design?" | B:explanation: budget = allowed lag; UI shows pending state until lag closed | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1604 | C/0.19/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does primitive-obsession leak domain rules?" | B:explanation: raw types (string id, decimal money) scatter rules across services; type-safe wrappers consolidate | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1605 | C/0.22/3.2] arc42 | A:"How does saga compensation order differ from forward order?" | B:explanation: compensations run in reverse — last successful step first | P:ARCH/3.2 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1606 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does threat-modelling using STRIDE classify threats?" | B:explanation: Spoof / Tamper / Repudiate / Info-disclose / DoS / Elevate-privilege | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1607 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does PASTA threat-modelling differ from STRIDE?" | B:explanation: PASTA = process-aligned 7 stages; STRIDE = checklist by attack class | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1608 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does data classification feed retention policy?" | B:explanation: class drives retention duration + encryption + access controls | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1609 | C/0.19/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does privacy threshold assessment trigger DPIA?" | B:explanation: PTA = preliminary; if high-risk, DPIA = full data protection impact analysis | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1610 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does defense-in-depth model layer controls?" | B:explanation: multiple independent controls — failure of one doesn't compromise system | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §26 — More observability + cross-domain (rows 1611-1650)

[CODEQ-1611 | C/0.20/3.5] otel | A:"How does observability differ from monitoring?" | B:explanation: monitoring = predefined dashboards/alerts; observability = explore unknowns via telemetry | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1612 | C/0.18/3.5] otel | A:"How do RED metrics differ from USE metrics?" | B:explanation: RED = Rate/Errors/Duration (services); USE = Utilisation/Saturation/Errors (resources) | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1613 | C/0.21/3.5] otel | A:"How does golden-signals model define the four signals?" | B:explanation: latency / traffic / errors / saturation — Google SRE four-signals model | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1614 | C/0.19/3.5] otel | A:"How does service-level indicator (SLI) differ from a metric?" | B:explanation: SLI = directly measures user-perceived behaviour; metric = any measurement (incl internal) | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1615 | C/0.22/3.5] otel | A:"How does W3C trace-context propagate across services?" | B:explanation: traceparent header + tracestate header; carries trace_id + span_id + flags | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1616 | C/0.20/3.5] otel | A:"How does baggage propagation differ from tracing context?" | B:explanation: baggage = arbitrary key/value across services; tracing context = id-only correlation | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1617 | C/0.18/3.5] otel | A:"How does OpenTelemetry handle resource attribute hierarchy?" | B:explanation: explicit > env > detector > SDK default; merge per spec rules | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1618 | C/0.21/3.5] otel | A:"How does an exemplar in metric instrumentation work?" | B:explanation: optional trace-id attached to metric data point; links series to specific trace | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1619 | C/0.19/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does multi-window multi-burn-rate alert work?" | B:explanation: 2-3 time windows (1h/6h/3d) with different burn rates; reduce false-positives | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1620 | C/0.22/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does adaptive sampling differ from fixed-rate?" | B:explanation: adaptive = adjusts rate dynamically based on load; fixed = constant rate | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1621 | C/0.20/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does tail-based sampling decide which traces to keep?" | B:explanation: collector waits for full trace; decision based on errors/latency criteria | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1622 | C/0.18/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does cardinality budget interact with metric design?" | B:explanation: high-card labels (user_id) explode storage; budget caps to maintain perf | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1623 | C/0.21/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does black-box monitoring differ from white-box?" | B:explanation: black-box = external probes; white-box = internal app metrics | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1624 | C/0.19/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does symptom-based alerting differ from cause-based?" | B:explanation: symptom = user-visible problem; cause = root condition; alert on symptoms | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1625 | C/0.22/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does on-call rotation reduce alert fatigue?" | B:explanation: load distribution + escalation paths + rotation cadence + post-incident review | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1626 | C/0.20/3.5] otel | A:"How does Core Web Vitals (LCP/INP/CLS) define UX perf?" | B:explanation: Largest Contentful Paint (load), Interaction to Next Paint, Cumulative Layout Shift | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1627 | C/0.18/3.5] otel | A:"How does Real-User Monitoring differ from synthetic?" | B:explanation: RUM = real users + actual telemetry; synthetic = scripted probe + fixed cadence | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1628 | C/0.21/3.5] otel | A:"How does service-mesh telemetry differ from app-instrumented telemetry?" | B:explanation: mesh = transport-level (L4/L7) + uniform; app = business semantics + custom | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1629 | C/0.19/3.5] otel | A:"How does observability-as-code platform unify alerts and dashboards?" | B:explanation: declarative spec; version-controlled; reviewed via PR; deployed via CI | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1630 | C/0.22/3.5] otel | A:"How does a trace differ from a span?" | B:explanation: trace = end-to-end request; span = single unit of work; trace contains spans | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1631 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does a security boundary differ from a trust zone?" | B:explanation: security boundary = enforced perimeter; trust zone = level of assumed integrity | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1632 | C/0.18/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does HTTP message signing prevent payload tampering in transit?" | B:explanation: HMAC over canonicalised request components; verifier rejects on mismatch | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1633 | C/0.21/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP token-binding mitigate stolen-token replay?" | B:explanation: token bound to client public key; proof signed per request; replay fails without key | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1634 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-9457 | A:"How does problem-detail's instance field differ from type?" | B:explanation: type = problem class URI (stable); instance = specific occurrence URI (per-request) | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1635 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does mutual-TLS differ from one-way TLS?" | B:explanation: one-way = server cert verified by client; mutual = both verify each other | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1636 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does field-level encryption protect PII at rest?" | B:explanation: per-field encryption with separate key; database sees ciphertext only | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1637 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does envelope encryption pattern reduce KMS calls?" | B:explanation: data key encrypts payload; KMS encrypts data key; one KMS call per payload | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1638 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does crypto-shredding enable GDPR right-to-erasure?" | B:explanation: delete encryption key; ciphertext becomes unrecoverable; equivalent to deletion | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1639 | C/0.19/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does WebAuthn passwordless flow work?" | B:explanation: registers public key on device; authenticator signs server challenge; server verifies | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1640 | C/0.22/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does passkey differ from WebAuthn credential?" | B:explanation: passkey = WebAuthn credential synced across devices via platform | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1641 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does HSTS preload protect against downgrade attack?" | B:explanation: browser hardcodes HTTPS-only domains; never falls back to plaintext | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1642 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does SameSite cookie attribute mitigate CSRF?" | B:explanation: SameSite=Lax/Strict prevents cookie on cross-site requests; eliminates CSRF vector | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1643 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does TLS 1.3 0-RTT differ from full handshake?" | B:explanation: 0-RTT replays prior session ticket; risk of replay attack on non-idempotent reqs | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1644 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does RFC 9421 derived component differ from header?" | B:explanation: derived = computed property like @method or @target-uri; header = HTTP header value | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1645 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP key-rotation work in long sessions?" | B:explanation: client must re-issue DPoP-bound token with new key; server requires re-auth on key change | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1646 | C/0.20/3.4] rfc-9457 | A:"How does problem detail interact with content negotiation?" | B:explanation: content type 'application/problem+json' or '+xml'; negotiate via Accept | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1647 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does FIDO2 server attestation verify authenticator?" | B:explanation: authenticator signs attestation; server checks against trusted-authenticator metadata | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1648 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does device-binding for high-risk operations work?" | B:explanation: enrol device fingerprint; tie token to device; require step-up on new device | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1649 | C/0.19/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does OAuth-app revocation cascade work?" | B:explanation: revoke triggers refresh-token revoke + access-token blacklist + downstream notify | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1650 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-7519 | A:"How does JWT encrypted (JWE) differ from signed (JWS)?" | B:explanation: JWS = signed but readable; JWE = encrypted (confidentiality + integrity) | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §27 — More multi-region + zero-trust + AI/ML internals (rows 1651-1690)

[CODEQ-1651 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does active-active multi-region differ from active-passive?" | B:explanation: active-active = both regions serve writes (needs conflict resolution); active-passive = standby waits for failover | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1652 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does conflict-free replicated data type (CRDT) enable active-active?" | B:explanation: data structure with mathematical merge function; concurrent updates always converge | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1653 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does cell-based architecture limit blast radius?" | B:explanation: traffic partitioned into cells; each cell isolated; failure stays within cell | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1654 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does identity-aware-proxy (IAP) differ from VPN?" | B:explanation: IAP = per-app authn/authz at L7; VPN = network-level tunnel | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1655 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does zero-trust differ from castle-and-moat security?" | B:explanation: zero-trust = verify every request regardless of source; castle-and-moat = trust internal network | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1656 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does eventual-consistency budget shape architecture?" | B:explanation: budget = allowed lag per read-path; design optimises for budget while preserving correctness | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1657 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does global rate-limiting differ from per-region?" | B:explanation: global = central token-bucket replicated; per-region = independent, fleet may exceed quota | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1658 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does data residency affect routing?" | B:explanation: per-tenant region pinning; routing layer enforces; audit ensures compliance | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1659 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does cross-region replication handle conflicts?" | B:explanation: timestamp-based last-write-wins, vector clocks, or operational transform; trade-offs differ | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1660 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does federated query work across multiple data warehouses?" | B:explanation: virtual layer + query plan + caching; pushes filters to source | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1661 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does FIDO2 phishing-resistance work?" | B:explanation: origin-bound credential; can't be reused on phishing site; cryptographic challenge | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1662 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does WebAuthn passkey sync across devices?" | B:explanation: passkey synced via platform (iCloud/Google) using end-to-end encryption | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1663 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does step-up authentication work?" | B:explanation: risk-score triggers stronger auth (MFA, biometric); only for high-stakes actions | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1664 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does post-quantum cryptography migration via hybrid scheme work?" | B:explanation: combines classical + PQ algorithms; resistant to both classical and quantum attacks | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1665 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does Confidential Computing (TEE) protect runtime data?" | B:explanation: hardware enclave isolates code/data from host OS; remote attestation verifies | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1666 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does zero-knowledge-proof verify without revealing?" | B:explanation: prover convinces verifier of statement truth without disclosing the secret | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1667 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does CRDT-based collaborative editor handle offline edits?" | B:explanation: each client's edits commute; merge on reconnect produces convergent state | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1668 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does outbox pattern guarantee event publication?" | B:explanation: write to outbox in same tx as state; relay reads + publishes; idempotent at consumer | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1669 | C/0.19/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does data-mesh's 'data as product' principle differ from data-as-asset?" | B:explanation: data-as-product = treated like external product (SLA, doc, owner); asset = internal artefact | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1670 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does feature store online-vs-offline serving differ?" | B:explanation: online = sub-ms low-latency for inference; offline = batch retrieval for training | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1671 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does vector DB ANN trade accuracy for latency?" | B:explanation: index (HNSW/IVF) sacrifices exact recall for sub-linear search; configurable trade-off | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1672 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does RAG embedding model choice affect retrieval quality?" | B:explanation: embedding determines similarity space; mismatched models degrade recall | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1673 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does ML drift detection differ from anomaly detection?" | B:explanation: drift = distribution shift over time; anomaly = single outlier event | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1675 | C/0.22/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does differential privacy add noise without breaking utility?" | B:explanation: calibrated noise based on epsilon; protects individuals while preserving aggregate stats | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1676 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI watermarking enable provenance tracking?" | B:explanation: embed signature in output (text/image); verify at consumption | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1677 | C/0.18/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI explainability differ from interpretability?" | B:explanation: interpretability = inherent (decision tree); explainability = post-hoc (SHAP/LIME) | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1678 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does chunking strategy affect RAG quality?" | B:explanation: chunk size + overlap + semantic boundaries trade context vs noise | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1681 | C/0.20/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does LLM jailbreak differ from prompt-injection?" | B:explanation: jailbreak removes safety guardrails; prompt-injection redirects task | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1683 | C/0.21/3.1] arc42 | A:"How does AI red-teaming differ from black-box testing?" | B:explanation: red-team probes safety boundaries adversarially; black-box tests behaviour without internals | P:ARCH/3.1 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1742 | C/0.18/3.4] istio-docs | A:"How does Istio Ambient Mesh differ from sidecar mode?" | B:explanation: ambient = ztunnel + waypoint; sidecar = per-pod proxy; ambient lower overhead | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1750 | C/0.22/2.1] dora-research | A:"How does SPACE framework differ from DORA?" | B:explanation: SPACE = 5 dims (satisfaction/perf/activity/comm/efficiency); DORA = 4 outcome metrics | P:PLAN/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §30 — More observability + security + functional + cross-domain (rows 1781-1850)

[CODEQ-1789 | C/0.19/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does adaptive sampling differ from fixed-rate?" | B:explanation: adaptive = adjusts rate dynamically based on load; fixed = constant rate | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-1790 | C/0.22/3.5] sre-book | A:"How does tail-based sampling decide which traces to keep?" | B:explanation: collector waits for full trace; decision based on errors/latency criteria | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1799 | C/0.19/3.5] otel | A:"How does a trace differ from a span?" | B:explanation: trace = end-to-end request; span = single unit of work; trace contains spans | P:ARCH/3.5 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-1806 | C/0.20/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does envelope encryption pattern reduce KMS calls?" | B:explanation: data key encrypts payload; KMS encrypts data key; one KMS call per payload | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

[CODEQ-2047 | C/0.18/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does device-binding for high-risk operations work?" | B:explanation: enrol device fingerprint; tie token to device; require step-up on new device | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2048 | C/0.21/3.4] arc42 | A:"How does OAuth-app revocation cascade work?" | B:explanation: revoke triggers refresh-token revoke + access-token blacklist + downstream notify | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2049 | C/0.19/3.4] rfc-9421 | A:"How does HTTP message signing prevent payload tampering?" | B:explanation: HMAC over canonicalised request components; verifier rejects on mismatch | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2050 | C/0.22/3.4] rfc-9449 | A:"How does DPoP token-binding mitigate stolen-token replay?" | B:explanation: token bound to client public key; proof signed per request; replay fails without key | P:ARCH/3.4 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]

## §66 — Project management internals + functional spec depth (200 rows; CODEQ-2051..CODEQ-2250)

[CODEQ-2051 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a RACI matrix differ from a DACI matrix?" | B:explanation: RACI = Responsible/Accountable/Consulted/Informed; DACI = Driver/Approver/Contributor/Informed; DACI emphasises decision velocity | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2052 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does a RAID log differ from a risk register?" | B:explanation: RAID = Risks/Assumptions/Issues/Dependencies; risk register holds only the R subset | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2053 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Monte-Carlo schedule simulation account for task uncertainty?" | B:explanation: samples PERT distributions per task; aggregates to project completion CDF; reports P50/P85 | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2054 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does WIP-limit kanban policy reduce cycle time?" | B:explanation: bounded WIP forces blockers visible; reduces multitasking; Little's law shrinks lead time | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2055 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does CFD (Cumulative Flow Diagram) expose process bottlenecks?" | B:explanation: widening band = WIP buildup; flat top = blocked work; reveals constraint stage | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2056 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Throughput-Forecasting differ from velocity-based forecasting?" | B:explanation: throughput = items/period (count); velocity = story-points; throughput forecasts via Monte-Carlo independent of estimates | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2057 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Cost-of-Delay quantification drive priority?" | B:explanation: weights value/time-criticality/risk-reduction; CoD/duration = WSJF; orders backlog | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2058 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does WSJF (Weighted-Shortest-Job-First) compute priority?" | B:explanation: (user-business value + time criticality + risk reduction) ÷ job duration | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2059 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Story-Mapping organise functional scope?" | B:explanation: backbone (user activity) + walking-skeleton slices; releases cut horizontally; depth = priority | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2060 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Event-Storming expose domain boundaries?" | B:explanation: orange events on timeline; aggregates emerge as command-event clusters; pivotal events become bounded-context lines | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2061 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Impact-Mapping connect deliverables to outcomes?" | B:explanation: goal → actor → impact → deliverable tree; deliverables only exist as paths to goal | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2062 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does User-Story-Splitting via SPIDR differ from horizontal slicing?" | B:explanation: SPIDR = Spike/Path/Interface/Data/Rule; preserves vertical end-to-end value | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2063 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Reference-Class-Forecasting reduce planning bias?" | B:explanation: ground estimate in distribution of similar past projects; corrects for inside-view optimism | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2064 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Pre-Mortem analysis surface hidden risk?" | B:explanation: assume failure has occurred; brainstorm causes backwards; converts unknowns into mitigations | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2065 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Five-Whys differ from Ishikawa fishbone?" | B:explanation: 5-Whys = linear causal chain; Ishikawa = categorised tree (Method/Machine/Material/etc.) | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2066 | C/0.20/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does FMEA (Failure-Mode-Effects-Analysis) prioritise risks?" | B:explanation: RPN = Severity × Occurrence × Detection; ranks failure modes for mitigation order | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2067 | C/0.18/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Bowtie-Analysis structure causal pathways?" | B:explanation: hazard centre; threats on left with preventive controls; consequences on right with recovery controls | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2068 | C/0.21/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does TARA (Threat-and-Risk-Assessment) differ from STRIDE?" | B:explanation: TARA = ISO/SAE 21434 automotive; STRIDE = Microsoft six categories; TARA scores impact + attack feasibility | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2069 | C/0.19/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does DPIA (Data-Protection-Impact-Assessment) sequence?" | B:explanation: necessity test → describe processing → assess risks → mitigations → consult DPO; mandated by GDPR Art-35 | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2070 | C/0.22/2.1] arc42 | A:"How does Architecture-Decision-Record (ADR) format prevent drift?" | B:explanation: Title/Status/Context/Decision/Consequences; immutable; new ADR supersedes; chains preserve intent | P:ARCH/2.1 plan | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2071 | C/0.20/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Jakob Nielsen's 10 heuristics structure UX evaluation?" | B:explanation: visibility/match/control/consistency/prevention/recognition/flexibility/aesthetic/recovery/help; severity-rated audit | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2072 | C/0.18/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Cognitive-Walkthrough differ from heuristic evaluation?" | B:explanation: walkthrough simulates first-time user task; heuristic = expert review against principles | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2073 | C/0.21/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does System-Usability-Scale (SUS) compute the score?" | B:explanation: 10 statements (5 positive/5 negative) on 5-pt; (∑ contributions) × 2.5; >68 = above-average | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2074 | C/0.19/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Net-Promoter-Score (NPS) categorise responses?" | B:explanation: 0-10 likely-to-recommend; promoters 9-10 minus detractors 0-6 = NPS; passives ignored | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2075 | C/0.22/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does HEART framework measure UX outcomes?" | B:explanation: Happiness/Engagement/Adoption/Retention/Task-success; Goals → Signals → Metrics for each | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2076 | C/0.20/3.6] aria-spec | A:"How does ARIA live-region politeness affect screen-reader announcement?" | B:explanation: polite waits for idle; assertive interrupts; off suppresses; per atomic/relevant attributes | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2077 | C/0.18/3.6] aria-spec | A:"How does ARIA roving-tabindex pattern handle keyboard nav inside composite widgets?" | B:explanation: only one element has tabindex=0; arrow keys move focus by changing tabindex; Tab leaves widget | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2078 | C/0.21/3.6] aria-spec | A:"How does ARIA-modal dialog trap focus correctly?" | B:explanation: focus on first focusable on open; Tab cycles within; Esc closes; restore focus on close | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2079 | C/0.19/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.2 success criterion 2.4.11 (focus-not-obscured) apply?" | B:explanation: focused element must not be entirely hidden by sticky header/footer/dialog | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2080 | C/0.22/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.7 (dragging-movements) apply?" | B:explanation: any drag operation must have single-pointer alternative (click+select); covers map pan, sliders | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2081 | C/0.20/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.2 SC 2.5.8 (target-size minimum) apply?" | B:explanation: pointer targets ≥24×24 CSS px or have spacing equivalent; prevents fat-finger errors | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2082 | C/0.18/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.2 SC 3.3.7 (redundant-entry) apply?" | B:explanation: information already provided in same process auto-filled or selectable; not re-entered | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2083 | C/0.21/3.6] wcag-2.2 | A:"How does WCAG 2.2 SC 3.3.8 (accessible-authentication) apply?" | B:explanation: cognitive function tests (memory/transcription) require alternative; passkeys exempt | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2084 | C/0.19/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Skeleton-screen pattern improve perceived performance?" | B:explanation: render greyed layout placeholders during fetch; user perceives progress vs blank/spinner | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2085 | C/0.22/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Optimistic-UI update differ from pessimistic?" | B:explanation: optimistic applies change locally before server confirm; rolls back on error; faster perceived | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2086 | C/0.20/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Stale-While-Revalidate (SWR) caching strategy work?" | B:explanation: serve cached immediately; revalidate in background; replace on fresh; user sees no spinner | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2087 | C/0.18/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Anchor-tag focus-ring suppression breach a11y?" | B:explanation: outline:none removes keyboard-user focus indicator; mouse user unaffected; fails WCAG 2.4.7 | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2088 | C/0.21/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does prefers-reduced-motion media query alter animation?" | B:explanation: respects OS-level vestibular preference; animations gated to short fades or disabled | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2089 | C/0.19/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does color-scheme CSS property differ from prefers-color-scheme?" | B:explanation: prefers-color-scheme = OS preference; color-scheme = component declares supported schemes | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2090 | C/0.22/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does container-query differ from media-query for component layouts?" | B:explanation: container-query targets ancestor size; component reusable in any context regardless of viewport | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2091 | C/0.20/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does view-transitions API enable cross-document animation?" | B:explanation: snapshot before/after; CSS animates pseudo-elements; navigation feels SPA-like | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2092 | C/0.18/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does CSS subgrid differ from grid in nested layouts?" | B:explanation: subgrid inherits parent grid tracks; nested items align with outer grid; gap inherited | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2093 | C/0.21/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does CSS @layer order resolve cascade conflicts?" | B:explanation: layers order overrides specificity within their layer; later layer wins regardless of specificity | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2094 | C/0.19/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does popover attribute differ from a custom modal?" | B:explanation: native popover handles top-layer/light-dismiss/focus management automatically; less custom JS | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2095 | C/0.22/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does dialog showModal versus open differ?" | B:explanation: showModal = top-layer + inert behind + Esc close; open = static inline render | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2096 | C/0.20/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does inert attribute affect descendant focusability?" | B:explanation: inert subtree removed from sequential focus + AT tree + click target; useful for behind-modal | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2097 | C/0.18/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does Intersection-Observer enable infinite scroll?" | B:explanation: sentinel near list bottom; observer fires when intersecting viewport; loads next page | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2098 | C/0.21/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does ResizeObserver differ from window resize event for components?" | B:explanation: per-element observer; debounced via rAF; component reacts to its own size, not viewport | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2099 | C/0.19/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does CSS contain property optimise rendering?" | B:explanation: hints isolation (layout/paint/style/size); browser skips work outside the boundary | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2100 | C/0.22/3.6] arc42 | A:"How does content-visibility:auto improve list rendering?" | B:explanation: skips off-screen rendering work; near viewport hydrates; saves layout/paint cycles | P:ARCH/3.6 design | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2101 | C/0.20/5.1] playwright | A:"How does Playwright trace-viewer reconstruct test failures?" | B:explanation: records DOM snapshots, network, console, sourcemaps; replays scrubbable timeline | P:ARCH/5.1 test | R:[WRONG: how-question]
[CODEQ-2102 | C/0.18/5.1] playwright | A:"How does Playwright auto-waiting differ from explicit waits?" | B:explanation: waits for actionability (visible/stable/enabled) before each action; no manual sleep | P:ARCH/5.1 test | R:[WRONG: how-question]
